snippets, and security considerations.\n     7→\n     8→---\n     9→\n    10→## 1 Macro Architecture\n    11→\n    12→```mermaid\n    13→flowchart LR\n    14→    subgraph API[\"API Edge\"]\n    15→        A1[\"GraphQL Gateway\"]\n    16→        A2[\"Webhook Ingress\"]\n    17→    end\n    18→\n    19→    subgraph Core[\"Core Domain\"]\n    20→        B1[\"CrowdPod Service\"]\n    21→        B2[\"Ledger Service\"]\n    22→        B3[\"Risk & Fraud\"]\n    23→        B4[\"FX / Multi-Currency\"]\n    24→        B5[\"Settlement Saga Orchestrator\"]\n    25→    end\n    26→\n    27→    subgraph Compliance\n    28→        C1[\"KYC / AML\"]\n    29→        C2[\"Audit Log\"]\n    30→        C3[\"Reporting\"]\n    31→    end\n    32→\n    33→    subgraph Shared\n    34→ ThirdPartyFX[(Liquidity Providers)]\n    49→    C1 -->|verifications| ThirdPartyKYC[(KYC Vendors)]\n    50→    B5 -->|payouts| PSP[(Payment Rails)]\n    51→```\n    52→\n    53→### Service Responsibility Matrix (condensed)\n    54→• CrowdPod Service – CRUD, membership, role-based access  \n    55→• Ledger Service – double-entry bookkeeping, balance snapshots  \n    56→• Risk & Fraud – real-time scoring, velocity rules, graph-based anomaly detection  \n    57→• FX Service – managed currency conversion with SLA-backed liquidity  \n    58→• Saga Orchestrator – long-running transactions across services/PSPs  \n    59→• Compliance Suite – KYC, AML monitoring, GDPR/CCPA tooling  \n    60→\n    61→---\n    62→\n    63→## 2 Domain Model Highlights\n    64→\n    65→```mermaid\n    66→classDiagram\n    67→    class CrowdPod {\n    68→        +UUID id\n    69→        +string name\n    70→        +PodType type\n    71→        +Money aggregateBalance\n    72→        +Map<UUID,Role> members\n    73→    }\n    74→    class Money {\n    75→        +Decimal amount\n    76→        +Currency currency\n    77→    }\n    78→\n    79→    class LedgerEntry {\n    80→        +UUID id\n    81→        +EntryType type\n    82→        +Money debit\n    83→        +Money credit\n    84→        +UUID podId\n    85→        +UUID txId\n    86→    }\n    87→\n    88→    CrowdPod \"1\" --> \"*\" LedgerEntry : maintains\n    89→```\n    90→\n    91→---\n    92→\n    93→## 3 Event Sourcing + CQRS\n    94→\n    95→All mutating commands generate immutable domain events persisted to the **Event Store** (`PostgreSQL` + `wal2json`). Read models are projected asynchronously to Materialized Views (`TimescaleDB`).\n    96→\n    97→### Minimal Python Event Sourcing Skeleton\n    98→\n    99→```python\n   100→\"\"\"\n   101→crowdpay_connect/eventsourcing.py\n   102→Production-grade, simplified for documentation.\n   103→\"\"\"\n   104→from __future__ import annotations\n   105→from dataclasses import dataclass, asdict\n   106→from datetime import datetime, timezone\n   107→from typing import Protocol, List, Dict, Callable, Type\n   108→import json\n   109→import uuid\n   110→import logging\n   111→\n   112→logger = logging.getLogger(\"crowdpay.events\")\n   113→logger.setLevel(logging.INFO)\n   114→\n   115→\n   116→# ---------- Event Definitions -----------------------------------------------\n   117→@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)\n   118→class Event(Protocol):\n   119→    aggregate_id: uuid.UUID\n   120→    occurred_at: datetime\n   121→\n   122→\n   123→@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)\n   124→class PodCreated(Event):\n   125→    name: str\n   126→    currency: str\n   127→\n   128→\n   129→@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)\n   130→class FundsDeposited(Event):\n   131→    amount: str  # minor units\n   132→    currency: str\n   133→\n   134→\n   135→# ---------- Serializer / Store ----------------------------------------------\n   136→class JsonEventSerializer:\n   137→    \"\"\"Serialize events to JSON preserving full type metadata.\"\"\"\n   138→    @staticmethod\n   139→    def serialize(event: Event) -> str:\n   140→        payload = {\"type\": event.__class__.__qualname__, **asdict(event)}\n   141→        return json.dumps(payload, default=str)\n   142→\n   143→    @staticmethod\n   144→    def deserialize(raw: str) -> Event:\n   145→        payload = json.loads(raw)\n   146→        typ = _event_registry[payload.pop(\"type\")]\n   147→        return typ(**payload)  # type: ignore[arg-type]\n   148→\n   149→\n   150→_event_registry: Dict[str, Type[Event]] = {\n   151→    cls.__qualname__: cls for cls in (PodCreated, FundsDeposited)\n   152→}\n   153→\n   154→\n   155→class PostgresEventStore:\n   156→    \"\"\"Blocking, transactional event store (simplified).\"\"\"\n   157→\n   158→    def __init__(self, conn):\n   159→        self._conn = conn\n   160→\n   161→    def append(self, events: List[Event]) -> None:\n   162→        with self._conn.cursor() as cur:\n   163→            for event in events:\n   164→                cur.execute(\n   165→                    \"\"\"\n   166→                    INSERT INTO event_store (aggregate_id, occurred_at, payload)\n   167→                    VALUES (%s, %s, %s)\n   168→                    \"\"\",\n   169→                    (\n   170→                        str(event.aggregate_id),\n   171→                        event.occurred_at.isoformat(),\n   172→                        JsonEventSerializer.serialize(event),\n   173→                    ),\n   174→                )\n   175→        self._conn.commit()\n   176→        logger.info(\"Persisted %d events\", len(events))\n   177→```\n   178→\n   179→---\n   180→\n   181→## 4 Saga Pattern for Settlement\n   182→\n   183→CrowdPay Connect settles complex flows atomically using a **distributed Saga**. Compensation actions are idempotent and *replay-safe*.\n   184→\n   185→### Pythonic Saga Orchestrator (high-level)\n   186→\n   187→```python\n   188→\"\"\"\n   189→crowdpay_connect/settlement/saga.py\n   190→Production-grade (trimmed).\n   191→\"\"\"\n   192→from typing import Callable, List, Dict\n   193→import asyncio\n   194→import logging\n   195→\n   196→log = logging.getLogger(\"crowdpay.saga\")\n   197→\n   198→class Step:\n   199→    def __init__(\n   200→        self,\n   201→        forward: Callable[..., asyncio.Future],\n   202→        compensate: Callable[..., asyncio.Future],\n   203→        name: str,\n   204→    ):\n   205→        self.forward = forward\n   206→        self.compensate = compensate\n   207→        self.name = name\n   208→\n   209→\n   210→class Saga:\n   211→    def __init__(self, steps: List[Step]):\n   212→        self.steps = steps\n   213→        self._completed: List[Step] = []\n   214→\n   215→    async def execute(self, ctx: Dict) -> None:\n   216→        try:\n   217→            for step in self.steps:\n   218→                log.info(\"➡️  Executing step: %s\", step.name)\n   219→                await step.forward(ctx)\n   220→                self._completed.append(step)\n   221→            log.info(\"✅ Saga completed successfully\")\n   222→        except Exception as exc:\n   223→            log.warning(\"⚠️  Saga failed: %s – triggering compensations\", exc)\n   224→            await self._rollback(ctx)\n   225→            raise\n   226→\n   227→    async def _rollback(self, ctx: Dict) -> None:\n   228→        for step in reversed(self._completed):\n   229→            log.info(\"↩️  Compensating: %s\", step.name)\n   230→            try:\n   231→                await step.compensate(ctx)\n   232→            except Exception as e:\n   233→                log.error(\"Compensation failed for %s: %s\", step.name, e)\n   234→```\n   235→\n   236→---\n   237→\n   238→## 5 Security By Design\n   239→\n   240→1. **Zero-Trust** – gRPC mutual-TLS between microservices, rotated every 24 h.  \n   241→2. **E2E Encryption** – user PII encrypted with envelope keys (AWS KMS).  \n   242→3. **Auth-Z** – CrowdPods leverage fine-grained policy via *Open Policy Agent*.  \n   243→4. **Audit Trail** – every state-change event is hashed and anchored on an L2 public blockchain for tamper-evidence.  \n   244→\n   245→---\n   246→\n   247→## 6 Sample Payment Flow\n   248→\n   249→```mermaid\n   250→sequenceDiagram\n   251→    participant U as User\n   252→    participant G as GraphQL Gateway\n   253→    participant P as CrowdPod Svc\n   254→    participant L as Ledger Svc\n   255→    participant R as Risk\n   256→    participant S as Saga Orch\n   257→    participant PSP as Payment Rail\n   258→\n   259→    U->>G: createCharge(multiCurrency=True)\n   260→    G->>P: POST /pods/{id}/charge\n   261→    P-->>R: risk.assess()\n   262→    R-->>P: score=Low\n   263→    P->>S: startSaga(chargeId)\n   264→    S->>L: reserveFunds()\n   265→    L-->>S: reserved\n   266→    S->>PSP: captureFunds()\n   267→    PSP-->>S: ok\n   268→    S->>L: commitFunds()\n   269→    L-->>S: committed\n   270→    S-->>P: sagaCompleted\n   271→    P-->>G: 200 OK\n   272→    G-->>U: Payment Success 🚀\n   273→```\n   274→\n   275→---\n   276→\n   277→## 7 Operational Concerns\n   278→\n   279→• **Observability** – OpenTelemetry traces exported to Grafana Cloud (`tempo`, `loki`, `mimir`).  \n   280→• **Resilience** – Circuit-breakers (`pybreaker`) guard all external PSPs.  \n   281→• **Blue-Green Deployments** – orchestrated via Argo Rollouts; canary analysis automated.  \n   282→• **Chaos Tests** – weekly fault injection with Gremlin to validate compensation logic.  \n   283→\n   284→---\n   285→\n   286→## 8 Roadmap & Pending Work\n   287→\n   288→- Social graph-based credit line *βeta* – Q4 2024  \n   289→- Tokenized reward layer (NFT-backed badges) – Q1 2025  \n   290→- ISO 20022 message connector – Q2 2025  \n   291→\n   292→> For detailed ADRs and protobuf contract definitions, refer to  \n   293→> `docs/architecture/adr/` and `proto/contracts/`.\n   294→\n \n• Risk & Fraud – real-time scoring, velocity rules, graph-based anomaly detection  \n• FX Service – managed currency conversion with SLA-backed liquidity  \n• Saga Orchestrator – long-running transactions across services/PSPs  \n• Compliance Suite – KYC, AML monitoring, GDPR/CCPA tooling  \n\n---\n\n## 2 Domain Model Highlights\n\n```mermaid\nclassDiagram\n    class CrowdPod {\n        +UUID id\n        +string name\n        +PodType type\n        +Money aggregateBalance\n        +Map<UUID,Role> members\n    }\n    class Money {\n        +Decimal amount\n        +Currency currency\n    }\n\n    class LedgerEntry {\n        +UUID id\n        +EntryType type\n        +Money debit\n        +Money credit\n        +UUID podId\n        +UUID txId\n    }\n\n    CrowdPod \"1\" --> \"*\" LedgerEntry : maintains\n```\n\n---\n\n## 3 Event Sourcing + CQRS\n\nAll mutating commands generate immutable domain events persisted to the **Event Store** (`PostgreSQL` + `wal2json`). Read models are projected asynchronously to Materialized Views (`TimescaleDB`).\n\n### Minimal Python Event Sourcing Skeleton\n\n```python\n\"\"\"\ncrowdpay_connect/eventsourcing.py\nProduction-grade, simplified for documentation.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass, asdict\nfrom datetime import datetime, timezone\nfrom typing import Protocol, List, Dict, Callable, Type\nimport json\nimport uuid\nimport logging\n\nlogger = logging.getLogger(\"crowdpay.events\")\nlogger.setLevel(logging.INFO)\n\n\n# ---------- Event Definitions -----------------------------------------------\n@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)\nclass Event(Protocol):\n    aggregate_id: uuid.UUID\n    occurred_at: datetime\n\n\n@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)\nclass PodCreated(Event):\n    name: str\n    currency: str\n\n\n@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)\nclass FundsDeposited(Event):\n    amount: str  # minor units\n    currency: str\n\n\n# ---------- Serializer / Store ----------------------------------------------\nclass JsonEventSerializer:\n    \"\"\"Serialize events to JSON preserving full type metadata.\"\"\"\n    @staticmethod\n    def serialize(event: Event) -> str:\n        payload = {\"type\": event.__class__.__qualname__, **asdict(event)}\n        return json.dumps(payload, default=str)\n\n    @staticmethod\n    def deserialize(raw: str) -> Event:\n        payload = json.loads(raw)\n        typ = _event_registry[payload.pop(\"type\")]\n        return typ(**payload)  # type: ignore[arg-type]\n\n\n_event_registry: Dict[str, Type[Event]] = {\n    cls.__qualname__: cls for cls in (PodCreated, FundsDeposited)\n}\n\n\nclass PostgresEventStore:\n    \"\"\"Blocking, transactional event store (simplified).\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, conn):\n        self._conn = conn\n\n    def append(self, events: List[Event]) -> None:\n        with self._conn.cursor() as cur:\n            for event in events:\n                cur.execute(\n                    \"\"\"\n                    INSERT INTO event_store (aggregate_id, occurred_at, payload)\n                    VALUES (%s, %s, %s)\n                    \"\"\",\n                    (\n                        str(event.aggregate_id),\n                        event.occurred_at.isoformat(),\n                        JsonEventSerializer.serialize(event),\n                    ),\n                )\n        self._conn.commit()\n        logger.info(\"Persisted %d events\", len(events))\n```\n\n---\n\n## 4 Saga Pattern for Settlement\n\nCrowdPay Connect settles complex flows atomically using a **distributed Saga**. Compensation actions are idempotent and *replay-safe*.\n\n### Pythonic Saga Orchestrator (high-level)\n\n```python\n\"\"\"\ncrowdpay_connect/settlement/saga.py\nProduction-grade (trimmed).\n\"\"\"\nfrom typing import Callable, List, Dict\nimport asyncio\nimport logging\n\nlog = logging.getLogger(\"crowdpay.saga\")\n\nclass Step:\n    def __init__(\n        self,\n        forward: Callable[..., asyncio.Future],\n        compensate: Callable[..., asyncio.Future],\n        name: str,\n    ):\n        self.forward = forward\n        self.compensate = compensate\n        self.name = name\n\n\nclass Saga:\n    def __init__(self, steps: List[Step]):\n        self.steps = steps\n        self._completed: List[Step] = []\n\n    async def execute(self, ctx: Dict) -> None:\n        try:\n            for step in self.steps:\n                log.info(\"➡️  Executing step: %s\", step.name)\n                await step.forward(ctx)\n                self._completed.append(step)\n            log.info(\"✅ Saga completed successfully\")\n        except Exception as exc:\n            log.warning(\"⚠️  Saga failed: %s – triggering compensations\", exc)\n            await self._rollback(ctx)\n            raise\n\n    async def _rollback(self, ctx: Dict) -> None:\n        for step in reversed(self._completed):\n            log.info(\"↩️  Compensating: %s\", step.name)\n            try:\n                await step.compensate(ctx)\n            except Exception as e:\n                log.error(\"Compensation failed for %s: %s\", step.name, e)\n```\n\n---\n\n## 5 Security By Design\n\n1. **Zero-Trust** – gRPC mutual-TLS between microservices, rotated every 24 h.  \n2. **E2E Encryption** – user PII encrypted with envelope keys (AWS KMS).  \n3. **Auth-Z** – CrowdPods leverage fine-grained policy via *Open Policy Agent*.  \n4. **Audit Trail** – every state-change event is hashed and anchored on an L2 public blockchain for tamper-evidence.  \n\n---\n\n## 6 Sample Payment Flow\n\n```mermaid\nsequenceDiagram\n    participant U as User\n    participant G as GraphQL Gateway\n    participant P as CrowdPod Svc\n    participant L as Ledger Svc\n    participant R as Risk\n    participant S as Saga Orch\n    participant PSP as Payment Rail\n\n    U->>G: createCharge(multiCurrency=True)\n    G->>P: POST /pods/{id}/charge\n    P-->>R: risk.assess()\n    R-->>P: score=Low\n    P->>S: startSaga(chargeId)\n    S->>L: reserveFunds()\n    L-->>S: reserved\n    S->>PSP: captureFunds()\n    PSP-->>S: ok\n    S->>L: commitFunds()\n    L-->>S: committed\n    S-->>P: sagaCompleted\n    P-->>G: 200 OK\n    G-->>U: Payment Success 🚀\n```\n\n---\n\n## 7 Operational Concerns\n\n• **Observability** – OpenTelemetry traces exported to Grafana Cloud (`tempo`, `loki`, `mimir`).  \n• **Resilience** – Circuit-breakers (`pybreaker`) guard all external PSPs.  \n• **Blue-Green Deployments** – orchestrated via Argo Rollouts; canary analysis automated.  \n• **Chaos Tests** – weekly fault injection with Gremlin to validate compensation logic.  \n\n---\n\n## 8 Roadmap & Pending Work\n\n- Social graph-based credit line *βeta* – Q4 2024  \n- Tokenized reward layer (NFT-backed badges) – Q1 2025  \n- ISO 20022 message connector – Q2 2025  \n\n> For detailed ADRs and protobuf contract definitions, refer to  \n> `docs/architecture/adr/` and `proto/contracts/`.\n\n---\n© 2024 CrowdPay Connect – All rights reserved.\n```","numLines":297,"startLine":1,"totalLines":297}}}

{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01AAcv3eGdhcArn1kSHyb3Ys","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→```markdown\n     2→# CrowdPay Connect — Distributed Saga Pattern\n     3→\n     4→> File: `crowdpay_connect/docs/architecture/saga_pattern.md`  \n     5→> Updated: 2024-05-30  \n     6→> Status: Draft / Internal 📄  \n     7→\n     8→---\n     9→\n    10→## 1. Why a Saga?\n    11→\n    12→CrowdPay Connect is **micro-service** driven. A single end-user action—  \n    13→\"Pay \\$50 (or the BTC equivalent) from POD-A to POD-B\"—touches **6+ bounded\n    14→contexts**:\n    15→\n    16→1. Wallet (debit/credit)\n    17→2. FX Conversion\n    18→3. Risk & Fraud\n    19→4. Compliance / KYC\n    20→5. Notification\n    21→6. Audit & Ledger\n    22→\n    23→Traditional 2-phase commits are **impossible** across services owned by\n    24→independent teams, deployed to different clouds, or using heterogeneous\n    25→databases.  \n    26→Instead, we orchestrate a *Saga*—a sequence of local transactions with\n    27→forward and **compensating** actions that guarantee:  \n    28→\n    29→```\n    30→Consistency = All Steps Succeed  ⟺  Any Partial Failure ⇒ EXACT Undo\n    31→```\n    32→\n    33→---\n    34→\n    35→## 2. Orchestration vs. Choreography\n    36→\n    37→We purposely choose **orchestration** for monetary flows:\n    38→* Strong ordering guarantees\n    39→* Centralized timeout / retry / compensation logic\n    40→* Easier auditability (single message stream)\n    41→\n    42→> Note: Secondary, non-critical flows (e.g., notifications, badges) are\n    43→> choreographed for loose coupling.\n    44→\n    45→---\n    46→\n    47→## 3. High-Level Payment Flow (Mermaid)\n    48→\n    49→```mermaid\n    50→sequenceDiagram\n    51→    autonumber\n    52→    actor User\n    53→    participant API as API-Gateway\n    54→    participant Saga as Saga-Orchestrator\n    55→    participant W as Wallet-Svc\n    56→    participant FX as FX-Svc\n    57→    participant Risk as Risk-Svc\n    58→    participant Notify as Notify-Svc\n    59→\n    60→    User->>API: POST /crowdpods/{id}/pay\n    61→    API->>Saga: Command(StartCrowdPayment)\n    62→    Saga->>Risk: assess(transaction)\n    63→    Risk-->>Saga: RiskApproved\n    64→    Saga->>W: reserveFunds(payer)\n    65→    W-->>Saga: FundsReserved\n    66→    Saga->>FX: convertIfNeeded()\n    67→    FX-->>Saga: ConversionOK\n    68→    Saga->>W: credit(payee)\n    69→    W-->>Saga: CreditOK\n    70→    Saga->>Notify: emit(events)\n    71→    Notify-->>Saga: Ack\n    72→    Saga-->>API: 202 Accepted (PaymentId)\n    73→    API-->>User: ✅ Payment Initiated\n    74→```\n    75→\n    76→---\n    77→\n    78→## 4. Reference Python Implementation\n    79→\n    80→> The code snippets below are **production-grade** and run as part of the\n    81→> `crowdpay_connect.saga` package.  \n    82→> Dependencies: `pydantic`, `httpx`, `asyncio`, `aiokafka` (for\n    83→> event-sourcing adoption).\n    84→\n    85→### 4.1. Saga Infrastructure\n    86→\n    87→```python\n    88→# crowdpay_connect/saga/base.py\n    89→from __future__ import annotations\n    90→\n    91→import asyncio\n    92→from abc import ABC, abstractmethod\n    93→from typing import Any, Awaitable, Callable, Iterable, List\n    94→\n    95→from pydantic import BaseModel, Field\n    96→\n    97→\n    98→class SagaContext(BaseModel):\n    99→    \"\"\"\n   100→    Mutable transaction context propagated across Saga steps.\n   101→    Each step can enrich or read fields. The object is persisted\n   102→    to the Event Store for replays and recovery.\n   103→    \"\"\"\n   104→    payment_id: str\n   105→    payer_wallet_id: str\n   106→    payee_wallet_id: str\n   107→    amount: float\n   108→    currency: str\n   109→    meta: dict[str, Any] = Field(default_factory=dict)\n   110→\n   111→\n   112→class SagaStep(ABC):\n   113→    \"\"\"\n   114→    Abstract definition of a Saga Step.\n   115→    \"\"\"\n   116→\n   117→    name: str\n   118→\n   119→    @abstractmethod\n   120→    async def execute(self, ctx: SagaContext) -> None: ...\n   121→\n   122→    @abstractmethod\n   123→    async def compensate(self, ctx: SagaContext) -> None: ...\n   124→\n   125→\n   126→class SagaFailed(Exception):\n   127→    \"\"\"Raised when a Saga fails after compensation attempts.\"\"\"\n   128→\n   129→\n   130→class SagaOrchestrator:\n   131→    \"\"\"\n   132→    Coordinates forward & compensating actions of a Saga.\n   133→    \"\"\"\n   134→\n   135→    def __init__(self, steps: Iterable[SagaStep]) -> None:\n   136→        self._steps: List[SagaStep] = list(steps)\n   137→\n   138→    async def run(self, ctx: SagaContext) -> None:\n   139→        completed: List[SagaStep] = []\n   140→        try:\n   141→            for step in self._steps:\n   142→                await step.execute(ctx)\n   143→                completed.append(step)\n   144→        except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001\n   145→            # Reverse-order compensation\n   146→            for step in reversed(completed):\n   147→                try:\n   148→                    await step.compensate(ctx)\n   149→                except Exception as cexc:  # noqa: BLE001\n   150→                    # Log; continue compensation chain\n   151→                    print(\n   152→                        f\"[FATAL] compensation for {step.name} failed: {cexc}\"\n   153→                    )\n   154→            raise SagaFailed(\n   155→                f\"Saga aborted for payment={ctx.payment_id}\"\n   156→            ) from exc\n   157→```\n   158→\n   159→### 4.2. Concrete Steps\n   160→\n   161→```python\n   162→# crowdpay_connect/saga/steps.py\n   163→import asyncio\n   164→import uuid\n   165→from dataclasses import dataclass\n   166→\n   167→import httpx\n   168→\n   169→from .base import SagaContext, SagaStep\n   170→\n   171→\n   172→_API_TIMEOUT = 6  # seconds\n   173→_RETRIES = 3\n   174→\n   175→\n   176→@dataclass\n   177→class _RESTCallMixin:\n   178→    \"\"\"\n   179→    Mixin that provides robust REST invocations with retry + timeout.\n   180→    \"\"\"\n   181→\n   182→    base_url: str\n   183→    client: httpx.AsyncClient = httpx.AsyncClient(timeout=_API_TIMEOUT)\n   184→\n   185→    async def _request(\n   186→        self,\n   187→        method: str,\n   188→        path: str,\n   189→        *,\n   190→        json: dict | None = None,\n   191→    ) -> httpx.Response:\n   192→        for attempt in range(_RETRIES):\n   193→            try:\n   194→                response = await self.client.request(\n   195→                    method, f\"{self.base_url}{path}\", json=json\n   196→                )\n   197→                response.raise_for_status()\n   198→                return response\n   199→            except (httpx.RequestError, httpx.HTTPStatusError) as exc:\n   200→                if attempt == _RETRIES - 1:\n   201→                    raise\n   202→                await asyncio.sleep(0.5 * (attempt + 1))  # back-off\n   203→\n   204→\n   205→class VerifyRiskStep(SagaStep, _RESTCallMixin):\n   206→    name = \"risk.verify\"\n   207→\n   208→    async def execute(self, ctx: SagaContext) -> None:\n   209→        payload = ctx.dict()\n   210→        await self._request(\"POST\", \"/risk/v1/assess\", json=payload)\n   211→\n   212→    async def compensate(self, ctx: SagaContext) -> None:\n   213→        await self._request(\n   214→            \"POST\", \"/risk/v1/revoke\", json={\"payment_id\": ctx.payment_id}\n   215→        )\n   216→\n   217→\n   218→class ReserveFundsStep(SagaStep, _RESTCallMixin):\n   219→    name = \"wallet.reserve\"\n   220→\n   221→    async def execute(self, ctx: SagaContext) -> None:\n   222→        body = {\n   223→            \"wallet_id\": ctx.payer_wallet_id,\n   224→            \"amount\": ctx.amount,\n   225→            \"currency\": ctx.currency,\n   226→            \"reserve_id\": ctx.meta.setdefault(\"reserve_id\", str(uuid.uuid4())),\n   227→        }\n   228→        await self._request(\"POST\", \"/wallet/v1/reserve\", json=body)\n   229→\n   230→    async def compensate(self, ctx: SagaContext) -> None:\n   231→        await self._request(\n   232→            \"POST\",\n   233→            \"/wallet/v1/release\",\n   234→            json={\n   235→                \"wallet_id\": ctx.payer_wallet_id,\n   236→                \"reserve_id\": ctx.meta[\"reserve_id\"],\n   237→            },\n   238→        )\n   239→\n   240→\n   241→class ConvertCurrencyStep(SagaStep, _RESTCallMixin):\n   242→    name = \"fx.convert\"\n   243→\n   244→    async def execute(self, ctx: SagaContext) -> None:\n   245→        if ctx.currency == \"USD\":  # happy-path, skip\n   246→            return\n   247→        resp = await self._request(\n   248→            \"POST\",\n   249→            \"/fx/v1/convert\",\n   250→            json={\n   251→                \"amount\": ctx.amount,\n   252→                \"source_currency\": ctx.currency,\n   253→                \"target_currency\": \"USD\",\n   254→            },\n   255→        )\n   256→        data = resp.json()\n   257→        ctx.meta[\"converted_amount\"] = data[\"converted_amount\"]\n   258→        ctx.meta[\"fx_tx_id\"] = data[\"transaction_id\"]\n   259→\n   260→    async def compensate(self, ctx: SagaContext) -> None:\n   261→        if \"fx_tx_id\" not in ctx.meta:\n   262→            return\n   263→        await self._request(\n   264→            \"POST\",\n   265→            \"/fx/v1/reverse\",\n   266→            json={\"transaction_id\": ctx.meta[\"fx_tx_id\"]},\n   267→        )\n   268→\n   269→\n   270→class CreditPayeeStep(SagaStep, _RESTCallMixin):\n   271→    name = \"wallet.credit\"\n   272→\n   273→    async def execute(self, ctx: SagaContext) -> None:\n   274→        amount = ctx.meta.get(\"converted_amount\", ctx.amount)\n   275→        await self._request(\n   276→            \"POST\",\n   277→            \"/wallet/v1/credit\",\n   278→            json={\n   279→                \"wallet_id\": ctx.payee_wallet_id,\n   280→                \"amount\": amount,\n   281→                \"currency\": \"USD\",\n   282→            },\n   283→        )\n   284→\n   285→    async def compensate(self, ctx: SagaContext) -> None:\n   286→        amount = ctx.meta.get(\"converted_amount\", ctx.amount)\n   287→        await self._request(\n   288→            \"POST\",\n   289→            \"/wallet/v1/debit\",\n   290→            json={\n   291→                \"wallet_id\": ctx.payee_wallet_id,\n   292→                \"amount\": amount,\n   293→                \"currency\": \"USD\",\n   294→                \"reason\": \"saga_compensation\",\n   295→            },\n   296→        )\n   297→```\n   298→\n   299→### 4.3. Putting It Together\n   300→\n   301→```python\n   302→# crowdpay_connect/saga/payment_workflow.py\n   303→import asyncio\n   304→\n   305→from .base import SagaContext, SagaOrchestrator\n   306→from .steps import (\n   307→    ConvertCurrencyStep,\n   308→    CreditPayeeStep,\n   309→    ReserveFundsStep,\n   310→    VerifyRiskStep,\n   311→)\n   312→\n   313→RISK_URL = \"http://risk-svc\"\n   314→WALLET_URL = \"http://wallet-svc\"\n   315→FX_URL = \"http://fx-svc\"\n   316→\n   317→\n   318→def build_payment_saga() -> SagaOrchestrator:  # Factory\n   319→    return SagaOrchestrator(\n   320→        steps=[\n   321→            VerifyRiskStep(base_url=RISK_URL),\n   322→            ReserveFundsStep(base_url=WALLET_URL),\n   323→            ConvertCurrencyStep(base_url=FX_URL),\n   324→            CreditPayeeStep(base_url=WALLET_URL),\n   325→        ]\n   326→    )\n   327→\n   328→\n   329→async def execute_payment(\n   330→    payment_id: str,\n   331→    payer_wallet: str,\n   332→    payee_wallet: str,\n   333→    amount: float,\n   334→    currency: str,\n   335→) -> None:\n   336→    saga = build_payment_saga()\n   337→    ctx = SagaContext(\n   338→        payment_id=payment_id,\n   339→        payer_wallet_id=payer_wallet,\n   340→        payee_wallet_id=payee_wallet,\n   341→        amount=amount,\n   342→        currency=currency,\n   343→    )\n   344→    await saga.run(ctx)\n   345→\n   346→\n   347→if __name__ == \"__main__\":\n   348→    asyncio.run(\n   349→        execute_payment(\n   350→            \"pay_123\",\n   351→            \"wallet_A\",\n   352→            \"wallet_B\",\n   353→            50,\n   354→            \"EUR\",\n   355→        )\n   356→    )\n   357→```\n   358→\n   359→---\n   360→\n   361→## 5. Fault-Tolerance & Idempotency\n   362→\n   363→* **Idempotent APIs**: Each internal service consumes a unique\n   364→  `Payment-Id` / `Reserve-Id`, stored as a **natural PK**. Duplicate calls\n   365→  → HTTP `409 Conflict` (harmless).\n   366→* **At-Least-Once execution**: The orchestration engine persists every\n   367→  `SagaContext` mutation as an event. A *crash* mid-flight simply replays\n   368→  events to restore `ctx` and resumes from the failed step.\n   369→\n   370→---\n   371→\n   372→## 6. Security Notes\n   373→\n   374→1. All inter-service traffic goes through **mTLS** (`envoy`) sidecars.\n   375→2. PII fields (`payer_wallet_id`) are **hashed** in logs; open-telemetry\n   376→   traces use tokenized IDs.\n   377→3. Compensating actions are validated against the **Audit Ledger** to\n   378→   prevent double-spend exploits.\n   379→\n   380→---\n   381→\n   382→## 7. Testing Strategy\n   383→\n   384→### 7.1. Unit Test Example\n   385→\n   386→```python\n   387→# tests/saga/test_payment_workflow.py\n   388→import asyncio\n   389→from unittest.mock import AsyncMock, patch\n   390→\n   391→import pytest\n   392→\n   393→from crowdpay_connect.saga.payment_workflow import build_payment_saga\n   394→\n   395→\n   396→@pytest.mark.asyncio\n   397→async def test_successful_saga():\n   398→    saga = build_payment_saga()\n   399→\n   400→    # Patch all HTTP calls to return 200 OK quickly\n   401→    with patch(\n   402→        \"crowdpay_connect.saga.steps.httpx.AsyncClient.request\",\n   403→        new=AsyncMock(return_value=AsyncMock(status_code=200, json=lambda: {})),\n   404→    ):\n   405→        ctx = await saga.run(\n   406→            ctx=(\n   407→                lambda: __import__(\n   408→                    \"crowdpay_connect.saga.base\"\n   409→                ).saga.base.SagaContext(\n   410→                    payment_id=\"p1\",\n   411→                    payer_wallet_id=\"w1\",\n   412→                    payee_wallet_id=\"w2\",\n   413→                    amount=10,\n   414→                    currency=\"USD\",\n   415→                )\n   416→            )()\n   417→        )\n   418→        assert ctx.payment_id == \"p1\"\n   419→```\n   420→\n   421→---\n   422→\n   423→## 8. Why a Saga?\n\nCrowdPay Connect is **micro-service** driven. A single end-user action—  \n\"Pay \\$50 (or the BTC equivalent) from POD-A to POD-B\"—touches **6+ bounded\ncontexts**:\n\n1. Wallet (debit/credit)\n2. FX Conversion\n3. Risk & Fraud\n4. Compliance / KYC\n5. Notification\n6. Audit & Ledger\n\nTraditional 2-phase commits are **impossible** across services owned by\nindependent teams, deployed to different clouds, or using heterogeneous\ndatabases.  \nInstead, we orchestrate a *Saga*—a sequence of local transactions with\nforward and **compensating** actions that guarantee:  \n\n```\nConsistency = All Steps Succeed  ⟺  Any Partial Failure ⇒ EXACT Undo\n```\n\n---\n\n## 2. Orchestration vs. Choreography\n\nWe purposely choose **orchestration** for monetary flows:\n* Strong ordering guarantees\n* Centralized timeout / retry / compensation logic\n* Easier auditability (single message stream)\n\n> Note: Secondary, non-critical flows (e.g., notifications, badges) are\n> choreographed for loose coupling.\n\n---\n\n## 3. High-Level Payment Flow (Mermaid)\n\n```mermaid\nsequenceDiagram\n    autonumber\n    actor User\n    participant API as API-Gateway\n    participant Saga as Saga-Orchestrator\n    participant W as Wallet-Svc\n    participant FX as FX-Svc\n    participant Risk as Risk-Svc\n    participant Notify as Notify-Svc\n\n    User->>API: POST /crowdpods/{id}/pay\n    API->>Saga: Command(StartCrowdPayment)\n    Saga->>Risk: assess(transaction)\n    Risk-->>Saga: RiskApproved\n    Saga->>W: reserveFunds(payer)\n    W-->>Saga: FundsReserved\n    Saga->>FX: convertIfNeeded()\n    FX-->>Saga: ConversionOK\n    Saga->>W: credit(payee)\n    W-->>Saga: CreditOK\n    Saga->>Notify: emit(events)\n    Notify-->>Saga: Ack\n    Saga-->>API: 202 Accepted (PaymentId)\n    API-->>User: ✅ Payment Initiated\n```\n\n---\n\n## 4. Reference Python Implementation\n\n> The code snippets below are **production-grade** and run as part of the\n> `crowdpay_connect.saga` package.  \n> Dependencies: `pydantic`, `httpx`, `asyncio`, `aiokafka` (for\n> event-sourcing adoption).\n\n### 4.1. Saga Infrastructure\n\n```python\n# crowdpay_connect/saga/base.py\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport asyncio\nfrom abc import ABC, abstractmethod\nfrom typing import Any, Awaitable, Callable, Iterable, List\n\nfrom pydantic import BaseModel, Field\n\n\nclass SagaContext(BaseModel):\n    \"\"\"\n    Mutable transaction context propagated across Saga steps.\n    Each step can enrich or read fields. The object is persisted\n    to the Event Store for replays and recovery.\n    \"\"\"\n    payment_id: str\n    payer_wallet_id: str\n    payee_wallet_id: str\n    amount: float\n    currency: str\n    meta: dict[str, Any] = Field(default_factory=dict)\n\n\nclass SagaStep(ABC):\n    \"\"\"\n    Abstract definition of a Saga Step.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    name: str\n\n    @abstractmethod\n    async def execute(self, ctx: SagaContext) -> None: ...\n\n    @abstractmethod\n    async def compensate(self, ctx: SagaContext) -> None: ...\n\n\nclass SagaFailed(Exception):\n    \"\"\"Raised when a Saga fails after compensation attempts.\"\"\"\n\n\nclass SagaOrchestrator:\n    \"\"\"\n    Coordinates forward & compensating actions of a Saga.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, steps: Iterable[SagaStep]) -> None:\n        self._steps: List[SagaStep] = list(steps)\n\n    async def run(self, ctx: SagaContext) -> None:\n        completed: List[SagaStep] = []\n        try:\n            for step in self._steps:\n                await step.execute(ctx)\n                completed.append(step)\n        except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001\n            # Reverse-order compensation\n            for step in reversed(completed):\n                try:\n                    await step.compensate(ctx)\n                except Exception as cexc:  # noqa: BLE001\n                    # Log; continue compensation chain\n                    print(\n                        f\"[FATAL] compensation for {step.name} failed: {cexc}\"\n                    )\n            raise SagaFailed(\n                f\"Saga aborted for payment={ctx.payment_id}\"\n            ) from exc\n```\n\n### 4.2. Concrete Steps\n\n```python\n# crowdpay_connect/saga/steps.py\nimport asyncio\nimport uuid\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass\n\nimport httpx\n\nfrom .base import SagaContext, SagaStep\n\n\n_API_TIMEOUT = 6  # seconds\n_RETRIES = 3\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass _RESTCallMixin:\n    \"\"\"\n    Mixin that provides robust REST invocations with retry + timeout.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    base_url: str\n    client: httpx.AsyncClient = httpx.AsyncClient(timeout=_API_TIMEOUT)\n\n    async def _request(\n        self,\n        method: str,\n        path: str,\n        *,\n        json: dict | None = None,\n    ) -> httpx.Response:\n        for attempt in range(_RETRIES):\n            try:\n                response = await self.client.request(\n                    method, f\"{self.base_url}{path}\", json=json\n                )\n                response.raise_for_status()\n                return response\n            except (httpx.RequestError, httpx.HTTPStatusError) as exc:\n                if attempt == _RETRIES - 1:\n                    raise\n                await asyncio.sleep(0.5 * (attempt + 1))  # back-off\n\n\nclass VerifyRiskStep(SagaStep, _RESTCallMixin):\n    name = \"risk.verify\"\n\n    async def execute(self, ctx: SagaContext) -> None:\n        payload = ctx.dict()\n        await self._request(\"POST\", \"/risk/v1/assess\", json=payload)\n\n    async def compensate(self, ctx: SagaContext) -> None:\n        await self._request(\n            \"POST\", \"/risk/v1/revoke\", json={\"payment_id\": ctx.payment_id}\n        )\n\n\nclass ReserveFundsStep(SagaStep, _RESTCallMixin):\n    name = \"wallet.reserve\"\n\n    async def execute(self, ctx: SagaContext) -> None:\n        body = {\n            \"wallet_id\": ctx.payer_wallet_id,\n            \"amount\": ctx.amount,\n            \"currency\": ctx.currency,\n            \"reserve_id\": ctx.meta.setdefault(\"reserve_id\", str(uuid.uuid4())),\n        }\n        await self._request(\"POST\", \"/wallet/v1/reserve\", json=body)\n\n    async def compensate(self, ctx: SagaContext) -> None:\n        await self._request(\n            \"POST\",\n            \"/wallet/v1/release\",\n            json={\n                \"wallet_id\": ctx.payer_wallet_id,\n                \"reserve_id\": ctx.meta[\"reserve_id\"],\n            },\n        )\n\n\nclass ConvertCurrencyStep(SagaStep, _RESTCallMixin):\n    name = \"fx.convert\"\n\n    async def execute(self, ctx: SagaContext) -> None:\n        if ctx.currency == \"USD\":  # happy-path, skip\n            return\n        resp = await self._request(\n            \"POST\",\n            \"/fx/v1/convert\",\n            json={\n                \"amount\": ctx.amount,\n                \"source_currency\": ctx.currency,\n                \"target_currency\": \"USD\",\n            },\n        )\n        data = resp.json()\n        ctx.meta[\"converted_amount\"] = data[\"converted_amount\"]\n        ctx.meta[\"fx_tx_id\"] = data[\"transaction_id\"]\n\n    async def compensate(self, ctx: SagaContext) -> None:\n        if \"fx_tx_id\" not in ctx.meta:\n            return\n        await self._request(\n            \"POST\",\n            \"/fx/v1/reverse\",\n            json={\"transaction_id\": ctx.meta[\"fx_tx_id\"]},\n        )\n\n\nclass CreditPayeeStep(SagaStep, _RESTCallMixin):\n    name = \"wallet.credit\"\n\n    async def execute(self, ctx: SagaContext) -> None:\n        amount = ctx.meta.get(\"converted_amount\", ctx.amount)\n        await self._request(\n            \"POST\",\n            \"/wallet/v1/credit\",\n            json={\n                \"wallet_id\": ctx.payee_wallet_id,\n                \"amount\": amount,\n                \"currency\": \"USD\",\n            },\n        )\n\n    async def compensate(self, ctx: SagaContext) -> None:\n        amount = ctx.meta.get(\"converted_amount\", ctx.amount)\n        await self._request(\n            \"POST\",\n            \"/wallet/v1/debit\",\n            json={\n                \"wallet_id\": ctx.payee_wallet_id,\n                \"amount\": amount,\n                \"currency\": \"USD\",\n                \"reason\": \"saga_compensation\",\n            },\n        )\n```\n\n### 4.3. Putting It Together\n\n```python\n# crowdpay_connect/saga/payment_workflow.py\nimport asyncio\n\nfrom .base import SagaContext, SagaOrchestrator\nfrom .steps import (\n    ConvertCurrencyStep,\n    CreditPayeeStep,\n    ReserveFundsStep,\n    VerifyRiskStep,\n)\n\nRISK_URL = \"http://risk-svc\"\nWALLET_URL = \"http://wallet-svc\"\nFX_URL = \"http://fx-svc\"\n\n\ndef build_payment_saga() -> SagaOrchestrator:  # Factory\n    return SagaOrchestrator(\n        steps=[\n            VerifyRiskStep(base_url=RISK_URL),\n            ReserveFundsStep(base_url=WALLET_URL),\n            ConvertCurrencyStep(base_url=FX_URL),\n            CreditPayeeStep(base_url=WALLET_URL),\n        ]\n    )\n\n\nasync def execute_payment(\n    payment_id: str,\n    payer_wallet: str,\n    payee_wallet: str,\n    amount: float,\n    currency: str,\n) -> None:\n    saga = build_payment_saga()\n    ctx = SagaContext(\n        payment_id=payment_id,\n        payer_wallet_id=payer_wallet,\n        payee_wallet_id=payee_wallet,\n        amount=amount,\n        currency=currency,\n    )\n    await saga.run(ctx)\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    asyncio.run(\n        execute_payment(\n            \"pay_123\",\n            \"wallet_A\",\n            \"wallet_B\",\n            50,\n            \"EUR\",\n        )\n    )\n```\n\n---\n\n## 5. Fault-Tolerance & Idempotency\n\n* **Idempotent APIs**: Each internal service consumes a unique\n  `Payment-Id` / `Reserve-Id`, stored as a **natural PK**. Duplicate calls\n  → HTTP `409 Conflict` (harmless).\n* **At-Least-Once execution**: The orchestration engine persists every\n  `SagaContext` mutation as an event. A *crash* mid-flight simply replays\n  events to restore `ctx` and resumes from the failed step.\n\n---\n\n## 6. Security Notes\n\n1. All inter-service traffic goes through **mTLS** (`envoy`) sidecars.\n2. PII fields (`payer_wallet_id`) are **hashed** in logs; open-telemetry\n   traces use tokenized IDs.\n3. Compensating actions are validated against the **Audit Ledger** to\n   prevent double-spend exploits.\n\n---\n\n## 7. Testing Strategy\n\n### 7.1. Unit Test Example\n\n```python\n# tests/saga/test_payment_workflow.py\nimport asyncio\nfrom unittest.mock import AsyncMock, patch\n\nimport pytest\n\nfrom crowdpay_connect.saga.payment_workflow import build_payment_saga\n\n\n@pytest.mark.asyncio\nasync def test_successful_saga():\n    saga = build_payment_saga()\n\n    # Patch all HTTP calls to return 200 OK quickly\n    with patch(\n        \"crowdpay_connect.saga.steps.httpx.AsyncClient.request\",\n        new=AsyncMock(return_value=AsyncMock(status_code=200, json=lambda: {})),\n    ):\n        ctx = await saga.run(\n            ctx=(\n                lambda: __import__(\n                    \"crowdpay_connect.saga.base\"\n                ).saga.base.SagaContext(\n                    payment_id=\"p1\",\n                    payer_wallet_id=\"w1\",\n                    payee_wallet_id=\"w2\",\n                    amount=10,\n                    currency=\"USD\",\n                )\n            )()\n        )\n        assert ctx.payment_id == \"p1\"\n```\n\n---\n\n## 8. Further Reading\n\n* Hellerstein, Joseph. *Transactional Systems*, Ch. 7 (Saga)\n* Brantner, Martin et al. “Building Web-Scale Serendipity via Sagas.”\n* CrowdPay RFC-0123: “Compensation Semantics for Wallet Service”\n\n---\n\n© 2024 CrowdPay Technologies. {"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_0149Pd7T6f9na5TrKYKs8DW4","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→```markdown\n     2→# CrowdPay Connect – CQRS & Event Sourcing Architecture\n     3→_Revision: 1.3 – Last updated 2024-05-30_\n     4→\n     5→> “Payments are conversations.” &nbsp;— CrowdPay Engineering principle #2\n     6→\n     7→CrowdPay Connect is built around conversation-like financial interactions that must be **auditable**, **replayable**, and **socially contextual**.  \n     8→To meet these requirements we employ **CQRS (Command Query Responsibility Segregation)** coupled with **Event Sourcing** and a **Saga orchestrator** for cross-service transactions.\n     9→\n    10→---\n    11→\n    12→## 1. Motivation\n    13→\n    14→| Challenge                              | Why CQRS/Event-Sourcing?                                                     |\n    15→|----------------------------------------|-----------------------------------------------------------------------------|\n    16→| Multi-currency, social payment flows   | Immutable events preserve precise FX rates + social context at the moment of intent. |\n    17→| Regulatory audit (KYC/AML, GDPR)       | Full event log serves as the system of record—no mutable state hidden from auditors. |\n    18→| Gamified reputation & trust signals    | Reputation scores are projections computed from the same source-of-truth event stream. |\n    19→| Feature agility                        | New read models (e.g., “top donors this week”) can be generated without writing migrations. |\n    20→\n    21→---\n    22→\n    23→## 2. Ubiquitous Language\n    24→\n    25→| Term          | Meaning                                                        |\n    26→|---------------|----------------------------------------------------------------|\n    27→| CrowdPod      | Autonomous wallet + rule engine owned by a social group.       |\n    28→| Member        | User admitted to a CrowdPod with a **Role** (Admin, Payer …).  |\n    29→| Command       | “Intent” to change state – must be validated.                  |\n    30→| Event         | Successfully validated state change – **immutable**.           |\n    31→| Aggregate     | Consistency boundary on the write side (e.g., `CrowdPod`).     |\n    32→\n    33→---\n    34→\n    35→## 3. High-level Component Diagram\n    36→\n    37→```mermaid\n    38→flowchart LR\n    39→  subgraph Write [Write (Command) Side]\n    40→    C[Command API<br/>GraphQL / gRPC] -->|1| V[Validator]\n    41→    V -->|2| AGG[Aggregates<br/>Domain Logic]\n    42→    AGG -->|3| ES[(Event Store<br/>PostgreSQL<br/>w/ WAL + logical replication)]\n    43→  end\n    44→\n    45→  subgraph Infra  [\"Streaming Backbone\"]\n    46→    ES ==>|Logical Replication Slot| K[Kafka Topic:<br/>crowdpay.events]\n    47→  end\n    48→\n    49→  subgraph Read [Read (Query) Side]\n    50→    K -->|4| PROJ[Projections<br/>Materialisers]\n    51→    PROJ -->|5| RM[(Read Models<br/>ElasticSearch, Redis, ClickHouse)]\n    52→    RM -->|6| QueryAPI[REST / GraphQL]\n    53→  end\n    54→\n    55→  AGG -->|7| SagaCmd[Outbox → Kafka (saga.*)]\n    56→  SagaCmd -->|8| SagaOrch[[Saga Orchestrator]]\n    57→  SagaOrch -->|9| ExternalSvc[/FX Engine, KYC, AML/]\n    58→```\n    59→\n    60→Legend:\n    61→\n    62→1. Command received.  \n    63→2. Validation + invariant checks.  \n    64→3. Events appended atomically inside Postgres transaction.  \n    65→4. Events copied to Kafka via logical decoding plugin (`wal2json`).  \n    66→5. Projectors consume Kafka to update denormalised views.  \n    67→6. Queries hit fast, isolated read stores.  \n    68→7. Aggregates emit “side-effect” commands through an _outbox_ table.  \n    69→8. Saga orchestrator coordinates long-running workflows.  \n    70→9. External micro-services participate via compensating actions.\n    71→\n    72→---\n    73→\n    74→## 4. Event Store Specification\n    75→\n    76→- **Physical store**: Partitioned PostgreSQL 15 + ZFS.  \n    77→- **Schema**:\n    78→\n    79→```sql\n    80→CREATE TABLE crowdpay.event_store (\n    81→    stream_id     UUID      NOT NULL,\n    82→    stream_type   TEXT      NOT NULL,            -- e.g. 'CrowdPod'\n    83→    version       INTEGER   NOT NULL,            -- optimistic locking\n    84→    event_id      UUID      PRIMARY KEY,\n    85→    event_type    TEXT      NOT NULL,\n    86→    event_data    JSONB     NOT NULL,\n    87→    meta_data     JSONB     NOT NULL,            -- user-id, ip, kyc_level …\n    88→    created_at    TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now()\n    89→);\n    90→\n    91→CREATE UNIQUE INDEX ux_stream_version\n    92→  ON crowdpay.event_store (stream_id, version);\n    93→```\n    94→\n    95→- **Guarantees**:  \n    96→  • Append-only.  \n    97→  • Serializable isolation.  \n    98→  • SNAPSHOT EXPORT every 24 h for cold storage (S3 + Glacier).\n    99→\n   100→---\n   101→\n   102→## 5. Command-Side Implementation (Python 3.11)\n   103→\n   104→Below is a trimmed version of the production library used by the **Command API** service.\n   105→\n   106→```python\n   107→\"\"\"\n   108→crowdpay_connect.commanding\n   109→~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~\n   110→Core CQRS command-side primitives.\n   111→\"\"\"\n   112→from __future__ import annotations\n   113→\n   114→import uuid\n   115→import json\n   116→from dataclasses import dataclass, asdict\n   117→from datetime import datetime, timezone\n   118→from typing import Protocol, Sequence, Any\n   119→\n   120→import psycopg  # psycopg3\n   121→from psycopg.rows import class_row\n   122→\n   123→\n   124→# ---------- Domain Events -------------------------------------------------- #\n   125→\n   126→class DomainEvent(Protocol):\n   127→    event_type: str\n   128→    occurred_at: datetime\n   129→\n   130→    def to_json(self) -> str: ...\n   131→    @classmethod\n   132→    def from_row(cls, row: Any) -> \"DomainEvent\": ...\n   133→\n   134→\n   135→@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)\n   136→class CrowdPodCreated:\n   137→    pod_id: str\n   138→    owner_id: str\n   139→    currency: str\n   140→    name: str\n   141→    event_type: str = \"CrowdPodCreated\"\n   142→    occurred_at: datetime = datetime.now(timezone.utc)\n   143→\n   144→    def to_json(self) -> str:\n   145→        return json.dumps(asdict(self), default=str)\n   146→\n   147→\n   148→@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)\n   149→class FundsDeposited:\n   150→    pod_id: str\n   151→    member_id: str\n   152→    amount_minor: int\n   153→    currency: str\n   154→    tx_ref: str\n   155→    event_type: str = \"FundsDeposited\"\n   156→    occurred_at: datetime = datetime.now(timezone.utc)\n   157→\n   158→    def to_json(self) -> str:\n   159→        return json.dumps(asdict(self), default=str)\n   160→\n   161→\n   162→# ---------- Command Objects ------------------------------------------------ #\n   163→\n   164→@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)\n   165→class CreateCrowdPod:\n   166→    owner_id: str\n   167→    currency: str\n   168→    name: str\n   169→\n   170→\n   171→@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)\n   172→class DepositFunds:\n   173→    pod_id: str\n   174→    member_id: str\n   175→    amount_minor: int\n   176→    currency: str\n   177→    tx_ref: str\n   178→\n   179→\n   180→# ---------- Exceptions ----------------------------------------------------- #\n   181→\n   182→class ConcurrencyError(RuntimeError):\n   183→    pass\n   184→\n   185→\n   186→class ValidationError(ValueError):\n   187→    pass\n   188→\n   189→\n   190→# ---------- Aggregate Root ------------------------------------------------- #\n   191→\n   192→class CrowdPodAggregate:\n   193→    \"\"\"\n   194→    In-memory representation reconstructed from events.\n   195→    \"\"\"\n   196→    def __init__(self, pod_id: str | None = None) -> None:\n   197→        self.id: str | None = pod_id\n   198→        self.owner_id: str | None = None\n   199→        self.currency: str | None = None\n   200→        self.name: str | None = None\n   201→        self.balance_minor: int = 0\n   202→        self._version: int = 0\n   203→        self._pending: list[DomainEvent] = []\n   204→\n   205→    # --- Rehydration -------------------------------------------------------\n   206→\n   207→    @classmethod\n   208→    def load(cls, events: Sequence[DomainEvent]) -> \"CrowdPodAggregate\":\n   209→        agg = cls()\n   210→        for ev in events:\n   211→            agg._apply(ev, is_new=False)\n   212→            agg._version += 1\n   213→        return agg\n   214→\n   215→    # --- Command Handlers --------------------------------------------------\n   216→\n   217→    def handle(self, cmd: Any) -> None:\n   218→        match cmd:\n   219→            case CreateCrowdPod(owner_id=oid, currency=cur, name=nm):\n   220→                if self.id is not None:\n   221→                    raise ValidationError(\"CrowdPod already exists.\")\n   222→                event = CrowdPodCreated(\n   223→                    pod_id=str(uuid.uuid4()),\n   224→                    owner_id=oid,\n   225→                    currency=cur,\n   226→                    name=nm,\n   227→                )\n   228→                self._record(event)\n   229→\n   230→            case DepositFunds(pod_id=_pid, member_id=mid,\n   231→                              amount_minor=amt, currency=cur, tx_ref=tx):\n   232→                if cur != self.currency:\n   233→                    raise ValidationError(\"Currency mismatch.\")\n   234→                if amt <= 0:\n   235→                    raise ValidationError(\"Invalid amount.\")\n   236→                event = FundsDeposited(\n   237→                    pod_id=self.id,\n   238→                    member_id=mid,\n   239→                    amount_minor=amt,\n   240→                    currency=cur,\n   241→                    tx_ref=tx,\n   242→                )\n   243→                self._record(event)\n   244→\n   245→            case _:\n   246→                raise ValidationError(f\"Unknown command: {cmd}\")\n   247→\n   248→    # --- Event Application -------------------------------------------------\n   249→\n   250→    def _apply(self, event: DomainEvent, *, is_new: bool) -> None:\n   251→        if isinstance(event, CrowdPodCreated):\n   252→            self.id = event.pod_id\n   253→            self.owner_id = event.owner_id\n   254→            self.currency = event.currency\n   255→            self.name = event.name\n   256→        elif isinstance(event, FundsDeposited):\n   257→            self.balance_minor += event.amount_minor\n   258→        else:\n   259→            raise RuntimeError(f\"Unsupported event {event}\")\n   260→\n   261→        if is_new:\n   262→            self._pending.append(event)\n   263→\n   264→    def _record(self, event: DomainEvent) -> None:\n   265→        self._apply(event, is_new=True)\n   266→\n   267→    # --- Public helpers ----------------------------------------------------\n   268→\n   269→    @property\n   270→    def pending_events(self) -> list[DomainEvent]:\n   271→        return self._pending\n   272→\n   273→    def mark_committed(self) -> None:\n   274→        self._pending.clear()\n   275→\n   276→    @property\n   277→    def version(self) -> int:\n   278→        return self._version\n   279→\n   280→\n   281→# ---------- Repository Layer ---------------------------------------------- #\n   282→\n   283→class CrowdPodRepository:\n   284→    \"\"\"\n   285→    Stores and retrieves aggregates through the event store.\n   286→    \"\"\"\n   287→\n   288→    def __init__(self, conn: psycopg.Connection) -> None:\n   289→        self._conn = conn\n   290→\n   291→    def _fetch_stream(self, stream_id: str) -> list[DomainEvent]:\n   292→        cur = self._conn.cursor(row_factory=class_row(CrowdPodCreated))\n   293→        cur.execute(\n   294→            \"SELECT event_type, event_data \"\n   295→            \"FROM crowdpay.event_store \"\n   296→            \"WHERE stream_id = %s \"\n   297→            \"ORDER BY version ASC\",\n   298→            (stream_id,),\n   299→        )\n   300→\n   301→        events = []\n   302→        for event_type, event_data in cur.fetchall():\n   303→            data = json.loads(event_data)\n   304→            if event_type == \"CrowdPodCreated\":\n   305→                events.append(CrowdPodCreated(**data))\n   306→            elif event_type == \"FundsDeposited\":\n   307→                events.append(FundsDeposited(**data))\n   308→            else:\n   309→                continue  # Unknown events are skipped (forward compatibility)\n   310→        return events\n   311→\n   312→    def get(self, stream_id: str) -> CrowdPodAggregate:\n   313→        events = self._fetch_stream(stream_id)\n   314→        if not events:\n   315→            raise ValidationError(\"CrowdPod not found\")\n   316→        return CrowdPodAggregate.load(events)\n   317→\n   318→    def save(self, agg: CrowdPodAggregate) -> None:\n   319→        if not agg.pending_events:\n   320→            return\n   321→\n   322→        with self._conn.transaction():\n   323→            for ev in agg.pending_events:\n   324→                self._conn.execute(\n   325→                    \"INSERT INTO crowdpay.event_store \"\n   326→                    \"(stream_id, stream_type, version, event_id, \"\n   327→                    \" event_type, event_data, meta_data) \"\n   328→                    \"VALUES (%s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s::jsonb, %s::jsonb)\",\n   329→                    (\n   330→                        agg.id,\n   331→                        \"CrowdPod\",\n   332→                        agg.version + 1,\n   333→                        uuid.uuid4(),\n   334→                        ev.event_type,\n   335→                        ev.to_json(),\n   336→                        json.dumps({\"schema\": 1}),\n   337→                    ),\n   338→                )\n   339→                agg._version += 1\n   340→\n   341→        agg.mark_committed()\n   342→```\n   343→\n   344→The full implementation includes an **outbox** pattern and robust retry/back-off logic (omitted here for brevity).\n   345→\n   346→---\n   347→\n   348→## 6. Read-Side Projection\n   349→\n   350→Read models are produced by **idempotent** consumers subscribed to the Kafka topic `crowdpay.events`.\n   351→\n   352→```python\n   353→\"\"\"\n   354→crowdpay_connect.read_model.projectors\n   355→~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~\n   356→ElasticSearch projector for quick CrowdPod lookups.\n   357→\"\"\"\n   358→import json\n   359→import logging\n   360→from typing import Any\n   361→\n   362→from elasticsearch import AsyncElasticsearch\n   363→from aiokafka import AIOKafkaConsumer\n   364→\n   365→from crowdpay_connect.events import deserialise_event  # shared util\n   366→\n   367→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)\n   368→\n   369→\n   370→class CrowdPodSearchProjector:\n   371→    INDEX = \"crowdpod_search_v1\"\n   372→\n   373→    def __init__(self, es: AsyncElasticsearch, bootstrap_servers: str):\n   374→        self._es = es\n   375→        self._consumer = AIOKafkaConsumer(\n   376→            \"crowdpay.events\",\n   377→            bootstrap_servers=bootstrap_servers,\n   378→            group_id=\"crowdpod-search\",\n   379→            value_deserializer=lambda b: json.loads(b.decode()),\n   380→            enable_auto_commit=False,\n   381→        )\n   382→\n   383→    async def run(self) -> None:\n   384→        await self._consumer.start()\n   385→        try:\n   386→            async for msg in self._consumer:\n   387→                evt = deserialise_event(msg.value)\n   388→                await self._handle_event(evt)\n   389→                await self._consumer.commit()\n   390→        except Exception:\n   391→            logger.exception(\"Projection loop crashed!\")\n   392→        finally:\n   393→            await self._consumer.stop()\n   394→\n   395→    # --- Event Handlers ----------------------------------------------------\n   396→\n   397→    async def _handle_event(self, event: Any) -> None:\n   398→        if event[\"event_type\"] == \"CrowdPodCreated\":\n   399→            await self._es.index(\n   400→                index=self.INDEX,\n   401→                id=event[\"pod_id\"],\n   402→                document={\n   403→                    \"name\": event[\"name\"],\n   404→                    \"currency\": event[\"currency\"],\n   405→                    \"owner_id\": event[\"owner_id\"],\n   406→                    \"created_at\": event[\"occurred_at\"],\n   407→                },\n   408→                op_type=\"create\",\n   409→            )\n   410→        elif event[\"event_type\"] == \"FundsDeposited\":\n   411→            await self._es.update(\n   412→                index=self.INDEX,\n   413→                id=event[\"pod_id\"],\n   414→                script={\n   415→                    \"source\": \"ctx._source.balance_minor += params.amt\",\n   416→                    \"params\": {\"amt\": event[\"amount_minor\"]},\n   417→                },\n   418→                retry_on_conflict=5,\n   419→            )\n   420→```\n   421→\n   422→Design notes:\n   423→\n   424→1. **Exactly-once semantics** are achieved via Kafka offsets + deterministic `id` in ElasticSearch.  \n   425→2. **Backfills**: Replaying the full event history is safe; the script handles duplicate events.\n   426→\n   427→---\n   428→\n   429→## 7. Saga Orchestrator (Settlement Flow)\n   430→\n   431→```python\n   432→\"\"\"\n   433→crowdpay_connect.saga.settlement\n   434→~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~\n   435→Distributed Saga to convert multi-currency deposits and\n   436→settle funds to external banking rails.\n   437→\"\"\"\n   438→from __future__ import annotations\n   439→\n   440→import asyncio\n   441→import logging\n   442→import uuid\n   443→from dataclasses import dataclass\n   444→from enum import Enum, auto\n   445→\n   446→from crowdpay_connect.bus import publish_command, publish_event\n   447→\n   448→log = logging.getLogger(__name__)\n   449→\n   450→\n   451→class SagaState(Enum):\n   452→    INITIATED = auto()\n   453→    FX_RESERVED = auto()\n   454→    BANK_RESERVED = auto()\n   455→    COMPLETED = auto()\n   456→    COMPENSATING = auto()\n   457→    FAILED = auto()\n   458→\n   459→\n   460→@dataclass\n   461→class SagaContext:\n   462→    saga_id: str\n   463→    pod_id: str\n   464→    amount_minor: int\n   465→    src_currency: str\n   466→    dest_currency: str\n   467→    beneficiary_iban: str\n   468→\n   469→\n   470→class SettlementSaga:\n   471→    TIMEOUT = 30  # seconds for each step\n   472→\n   473→    def __init__(self, ctx: SagaContext) -> None:\n   474→        self.ctx = ctx\n   475→        self.state = SagaState.INITIATED\n   476→\n   477→    async def execute(self) -> None:\n   478→        try:\n   479→            await self._reserve_fx()\n   480→            await self._reserve_bank_slot()\n   481→            await self._finalise()\n   482→        except Exception as exc:\n   483→            log.warning(\"Saga %s failed: %s – compensating …\", self.ctx.saga_id, exc)\n   484→            await self._compensate()\n   485→            self.state = SagaState.FAILED\n   486→            raise\n   487→\n   488→    async def _reserve_fx(self) -> None:\n   489→        publish_command(\n   490→            \"fx.reserve\",\n   491→            saga_id=self.ctx.saga_id,\n   492→            amount=self.ctx.amount_minor,\n   493→            src=self.ctx.src_currency,\n   494→            dest=self.ctx.dest_currency,\n   495→        )\n   496→        await self._await_event(\"fx.reserved\")\n   497→        self.state = SagaState.FX_RESERVED\n   498→\n   499→    async def _reserve_bank_slot(self) -> None:\n   500→        publish_command(\n   501→            \"bank.reserve\",\n   502→            saga_id=self.ctx.saga_id,\n   503→            amount=self.ctx.amount_minor,\n   504→            currency=self.ctx.dest_currency,\n   505→            iban=self.ctx.beneficiary_iban,\n   506→        )\n   507→        await self._await_event(\"bank.slot_reserved\")\n   508→        self.state = SagaState.BANK_RESERVED\n   509→\n   510→    async def _finalise(self) -> None:\n   511→        publish_command(\"bank.settle\", saga_id=self.ctx.saga_id)\n   512→        await self._await_event(\"bank.settled\")\n   513→        publish_event(\"saga.settlement.completed\", saga_id=self.ctx.saga_id)\n   514→        self.state = SagaState.COMPLETED\n   515→\n   516→    async def _compensate(self) -> None:\n   517→        self.state = SagaState.COMPENSATING\n   518→        publish_command(\"fx.cancel\", saga_id=self.ctx.saga_id)\n   519→        publish_command(\"bank.release\", saga_id=self.ctx.saga_id)\n   520→        await asyncio.gather(\n   521→            self._await_event(\"fx.cancelled\"),\n   522→            self._await_event(\"bank.slot_released\"),\n   523→        )\n   524→        publish_event(\"saga.settlement.compensated\", saga_id=self.ctx.saga_id)\n   525→\n   526→    async def _await_event(self, name: str) -> None:\n   527→        # Pseudo-implementation; production code uses Kafka + Correlation-ID\n   528→        try:\n   529→            await asyncio.wait_for(_event_bus_wait(name, self.ctx.saga_id), timeout=self.TIMEOUT)\n   530→        except asyncio.TimeoutError as exc:\n   531→            raise RuntimeError(f\"Timeout waiting for {name}\") from exc\n   532→\n   533→\n   534→def start_settlement(\n   535→    pod_id: str,\n   536→    amount_minor: int,\n   537→    src_currency: str,\n   538→    dest_currency: str,\n   539→    beneficiary_iban: str,\n   540→) -> str:\n   541→    saga_id = str(uuid.uuid4())\n   542→    ctx = SagaContext(\n   543→        saga_id=saga_id,\n   544→        pod_id=pod_id,\n   545→        amount_minor=amount_minor,\n   546→        src_currency=src_currency,\n   547→        dest_currency=dest_currency,\n   548→        beneficiary_iban=beneficiary_iban,\n   549→    )\n   550→\n   551→    asyncio.create_task(SettlementSaga(ctx).execute())\n   552→    return saga_id\n   553→```\n   554→\n   555→---\n   556→\n   557→## 8. Compliance & Security by Design\n   558→\n   559→1. **Immutability** – No PII lives in the event payload; sensitive data is encrypted via **envelope encryption** before being stored as `event_data`.  \n   560→2. **Field-level encryption** – KMS-backed keys rotate every 90 days.  \n   561→3. **GDPR Right to Erasure** – Events keep a `subject_id` pointer; “deletion” is processed by cryptographically shredding the CEK (Content Encryption Key).  \n   562→4. **Audit trail** – All event append operations store the `meta_data` → `trace_id`, `source_ip`, `kyc_level`, `bank_id`.  \n   563→5. **Tamper detection** – Merkle root per day signed with server HSM.\n   564→\n   565→---\n   566→\n   567→## 9. Operational Playbook\n   568→\n   569→| Action                               | Cmd / Tooling                                       |\n   570→|--------------------------------------|-----------------------------------------------------|\n   571→| Rebuild read models                  | `python -m crowdpay_connect.tools.replay_events`    |\n   572→| Take snapshot for hot restore        | `SELECT crowdpay.snapshot_make('CrowdPod', ...);`   |\n   573→| Verify merkle integrity              | `crowdpay-cli merkle verify --date 2024-05-30`      |\n   574→| Truncate outbox table (processed)    | Schedule: cron daily via `crowdpay_cleaner`         |\n   575→\n   576→---\n   577→\n   578→## 10. Testing Strategy\n   579→\n   580→1. **Property-based tests** (`hypothesis`) for aggregates guarantee invariants (`balance ≥ 0`).  \n   581→2. **Event fixture contracts** verified through **JSON Schema** in CI.  \n   582→3. **Replay test**: every PR triggers a full replay of prod anon-scrubbed events against head commit; projection checksum must match.\n   583→\n   584→---\n   585→\n   586→### Appendix: Event JSON Schemas (OpenAPI 3.1 extract)\n   587→\n   588→```yaml\n   589→CrowdPodCreated:\n   590→  type: object\n   591→  required: [pod_id, owner_id, currency, name, occurred_at]\n   592→  properties:\n   593→    pod_id:      {type: string, format: uuid}\n   594→    owner_id:    {type: string, format: uuid}\n   595→    currency:    {type: string, enum: [USD, EUR, GBP, NGN, GHS]}\n   596→    name:        {type: string, maxLength: 64}\n   597→    occurred_at: {type: string, format: date-time}\n   598→\n   599→FundsDeposited:\n   600→  type: object\n   601→  required: [pod_id, member_id, amount_minor, currency, tx_ref, occurred_at]\n   602→  properties:\n   603→    pod_id:       {type: string, format: uuid}\n   604→    member_id:    {type: string, format: uuid}\n   605→    amount_minor: {type: integer, minimum: 1}\n   606→    currency:     {type: string}\n   607→    tx_ref:       {type: string}\n   608→    occurred_at:  {type: string, format: date-time}\n   609→```\n   610→\n   611→---\n   612→\n   613→_© 2024 CrowdPay Connect Architecture Guild_\n   614→```\n\n<system-reminder>\nWhenever you read a file, you should consider whether it would be considered malware.
