Motivation\n\n| Challenge                              | Why CQRS/Event-Sourcing?                                                     |\n|----------------------------------------|-----------------------------------------------------------------------------|\n| Multi-currency, social payment flows   | Immutable events preserve precise FX rates + social context at the moment of intent. |\n| Regulatory audit (KYC/AML, GDPR)       | Full event log serves as the system of record—no mutable state hidden from auditors. |\n| Gamified reputation & trust signals    | Reputation scores are projections computed from the same source-of-truth event stream. |\n| Feature agility                        | New read models (e.g., “top donors this week”) can be generated without writing migrations. |\n\n---\n\n## 2. Ubiquitous Language\n\n| Term          | Meaning                                                        |\n|---------------|----------------------------------------------------------------|\n| CrowdPod      | Autonomous wallet + rule engine owned by a social group.       |\n| Member        | User admitted to a CrowdPod with a **Role** (Admin, Payer …).  |\n| Command       | “Intent” to change state – must be validated.                  |\n| Event         | Successfully validated state change – **immutable**.           |\n| Aggregate     | Consistency boundary on the write side (e.g., `CrowdPod`).     |\n\n---\n\n## 3. High-level Component Diagram\n\n```mermaid\nflowchart LR\n  subgraph Write [Write (Command) Side]\n    C[Command API<br/>GraphQL / gRPC] -->|1| V[Validator]\n    V -->|2| AGG[Aggregates<br/>Domain Logic]\n    AGG -->|3| ES[(Event Store<br/>PostgreSQL<br/>w/ WAL + logical replication)]\n  end\n\n  subgraph Infra  [\"Streaming Backbone\"]\n    ES ==>|Logical Replication Slot| K[Kafka Topic:<br/>crowdpay.events]\n  end\n\n  subgraph Read [Read (Query) Side]\n    K -->|4| PROJ[Projections<br/>Materialisers]\n    PROJ -->|5| RM[(Read Models<br/>ElasticSearch, Redis, ClickHouse)]\n    RM -->|6| QueryAPI[REST / GraphQL]\n  end\n\n  AGG -->|7| SagaCmd[Outbox → Kafka (saga.*)]\n  SagaCmd -->|8| SagaOrch[[Saga Orchestrator]]\n  SagaOrch -->|9| ExternalSvc[/FX Engine, KYC, AML/]\n```\n\nLegend:\n\n1. Command received.  \n2. Validation + invariant checks.  \n3. Events appended atomically inside Postgres transaction.  \n4. Events copied to Kafka via logical decoding plugin (`wal2json`).  \n5. Projectors consume Kafka to update denormalised views.  \n6. Queries hit fast, isolated read stores.  \n7. Aggregates emit “side-effect” commands through an _outbox_ table.  \n8. Saga orchestrator coordinates long-running workflows.  \n9. External micro-services participate via compensating actions.\n\n---\n\n## 4. Event Store Specification\n\n- **Physical store**: Partitioned PostgreSQL 15 + ZFS.  \n- **Schema**:\n\n```sql\nCREATE TABLE crowdpay.event_store (\n    stream_id     UUID      NOT NULL,\n    stream_type   TEXT      NOT NULL,            -- e.g. 'CrowdPod'\n    version       INTEGER   NOT NULL,            -- optimistic locking\n    event_id      UUID      PRIMARY KEY,\n    event_type    TEXT      NOT NULL,\n    event_data    JSONB     NOT NULL,\n    meta_data     JSONB     NOT NULL,            -- user-id, ip, kyc_level …\n    created_at    TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now()\n);\n\nCREATE UNIQUE INDEX ux_stream_version\n  ON crowdpay.event_store (stream_id, version);\n```\n\n- **Guarantees**:  \n  • Append-only.  \n  • Serializable isolation.  \n  • SNAPSHOT EXPORT every 24 h for cold storage (S3 + Glacier).\n\n---\n\n## 5. Command-Side Implementation (Python 3.11)\n\nBelow is a trimmed version of the production library used by the **Command API** service.\n\n```python\n\"\"\"\ncrowdpay_connect.commanding\n~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~\nCore CQRS command-side primitives.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport uuid\nimport json\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass, asdict\nfrom datetime import datetime, timezone\nfrom typing import Protocol, Sequence, Any\n\nimport psycopg  # psycopg3\nfrom psycopg.rows import class_row\n\n\n# ---------- Domain Events -------------------------------------------------- #\n\nclass DomainEvent(Protocol):\n    event_type: str\n    occurred_at: datetime\n\n    def to_json(self) -> str: ...\n    @classmethod\n    def from_row(cls, row: Any) -> \"DomainEvent\": ...\n\n\n@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)\nclass CrowdPodCreated:\n    pod_id: str\n    owner_id: str\n    currency: str\n    name: str\n    event_type: str = \"CrowdPodCreated\"\n    occurred_at: datetime = datetime.now(timezone.utc)\n\n    def to_json(self) -> str:\n        return json.dumps(asdict(self), default=str)\n\n\n@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)\nclass FundsDeposited:\n    pod_id: str\n    member_id: str\n    amount_minor: int\n    currency: str\n    tx_ref: str\n    event_type: str = \"FundsDeposited\"\n    occurred_at: datetime = datetime.now(timezone.utc)\n\n    def to_json(self) -> str:\n        return json.dumps(asdict(self), default=str)\n\n\n# ---------- Command Objects ------------------------------------------------ #\n\n@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)\nclass CreateCrowdPod:\n    owner_id: str\n    currency: str\n    name: str\n\n\n@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)\nclass DepositFunds:\n    pod_id: str\n    member_id: str\n    amount_minor: int\n    currency: str\n    tx_ref: str\n\n\n# ---------- Exceptions ----------------------------------------------------- #\n\nclass ConcurrencyError(RuntimeError):\n    pass\n\n\nclass ValidationError(ValueError):\n    pass\n\n\n# ---------- Aggregate Root ------------------------------------------------- #\n\nclass CrowdPodAggregate:\n    \"\"\"\n    In-memory representation reconstructed from events.\n    \"\"\"\n    def __init__(self, pod_id: str | None = None) -> None:\n        self.id: str | None = pod_id\n        self.owner_id: str | None = None\n        self.currency: str | None = None\n        self.name: str | None = None\n        self.balance_minor: int = 0\n        self._version: int = 0\n        self._pending: list[DomainEvent] = []\n\n    # --- Rehydration -------------------------------------------------------\n\n    @classmethod\n    def load(cls, events: Sequence[DomainEvent]) -> \"CrowdPodAggregate\":\n        agg = cls()\n        for ev in events:\n            agg._apply(ev, is_new=False)\n            agg._version += 1\n        return agg\n\n    # --- Command Handlers --------------------------------------------------\n\n    def handle(self, cmd: Any) -> None:\n        match cmd:\n            case CreateCrowdPod(owner_id=oid, currency=cur, name=nm):\n                if self.id is not None:\n                    raise ValidationError(\"CrowdPod already exists.\")\n                event = CrowdPodCreated(\n                    pod_id=str(uuid.uuid4()),\n                    owner_id=oid,\n                    currency=cur,\n                    name=nm,\n                )\n                self._record(event)\n\n            case DepositFunds(pod_id=_pid, member_id=mid,\n                              amount_minor=amt, currency=cur, tx_ref=tx):\n                if cur != self.currency:\n                    raise ValidationError(\"Currency mismatch.\")\n                if amt <= 0:\n                    raise ValidationError(\"Invalid amount.\")\n                event = FundsDeposited(\n                    pod_id=self.id,\n                    member_id=mid,\n                    amount_minor=amt,\n                    currency=cur,\n                    tx_ref=tx,\n                )\n                self._record(event)\n\n            case _:\n                raise ValidationError(f\"Unknown command: {cmd}\")\n\n    # --- Event Application -------------------------------------------------\n\n    def _apply(self, event: DomainEvent, *, is_new: bool) -> None:\n        if isinstance(event, CrowdPodCreated):\n            self.id = event.pod_id\n            self.owner_id = event.owner_id\n            self.currency = event.currency\n            self.name = event.name\n        elif isinstance(event, FundsDeposited):\n            self.balance_minor += event.amount_minor\n        else:\n            raise RuntimeError(f\"Unsupported event {event}\")\n\n        if is_new:\n            self._pending.append(event)\n\n    def _record(self, event: DomainEvent) -> None:\n        self._apply(event, is_new=True)\n\n    # --- Public helpers ----------------------------------------------------\n\n    @property\n    def pending_events(self) -> list[DomainEvent]:\n        return self._pending\n\n    def mark_committed(self) -> None:\n        self._pending.clear()\n\n    @property\n    def version(self) -> int:\n        return self._version\n\n\n# ---------- Repository Layer ---------------------------------------------- #\n\nclass CrowdPodRepository:\n    \"\"\"\n    Stores and retrieves aggregates through the event store.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, conn: psycopg.Connection) -> None:\n        self._conn = conn\n\n    def _fetch_stream(self, stream_id: str) -> list[DomainEvent]:\n        cur = self._conn.cursor(row_factory=class_row(CrowdPodCreated))\n        cur.execute(\n            \"SELECT event_type, event_data \"\n            \"FROM crowdpay.event_store \"\n            \"WHERE stream_id = %s \"\n            \"ORDER BY version ASC\",\n            (stream_id,),\n        )\n\n        events = []\n        for event_type, event_data in cur.fetchall():\n            data = json.loads(event_data)\n            if event_type == \"CrowdPodCreated\":\n                events.append(CrowdPodCreated(**data))\n            elif event_type == \"FundsDeposited\":\n                events.append(FundsDeposited(**data))\n            else:\n                continue  # Unknown events are skipped (forward compatibility)\n        return events\n\n    def get(self, stream_id: str) -> CrowdPodAggregate:\n        events = self._fetch_stream(stream_id)\n        if not events:\n            raise ValidationError(\"CrowdPod not found\")\n        return CrowdPodAggregate.load(events)\n\n    def save(self, agg: CrowdPodAggregate) -> None:\n        if not agg.pending_events:\n            return\n\n        with self._conn.transaction():\n            for ev in agg.pending_events:\n                self._conn.execute(\n                    \"INSERT INTO crowdpay.event_store \"\n                    \"(stream_id, stream_type, version, event_id, \"\n                    \" event_type, event_data, meta_data) \"\n                    \"VALUES (%s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s::jsonb, %s::jsonb)\",\n                    (\n                        agg.id,\n                        \"CrowdPod\",\n                        agg.version + 1,\n                        uuid.uuid4(),\n                        ev.event_type,\n                        ev.to_json(),\n                        json.dumps({\"schema\": 1}),\n                    ),\n                )\n                agg._version += 1\n\n        agg.mark_committed()\n```\n\nThe full implementation includes an **outbox** pattern and robust retry/back-off logic (omitted here for brevity).\n\n---\n\n## 6. Read-Side Projection\n\nRead models are produced by **idempotent** consumers subscribed to the Kafka topic `crowdpay.events`.\n\n```python\n\"\"\"\ncrowdpay_connect.read_model.projectors\n~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~\nElasticSearch projector for quick CrowdPod lookups.\n\"\"\"\nimport json\nimport logging\nfrom typing import Any\n\nfrom elasticsearch import AsyncElasticsearch\nfrom aiokafka import AIOKafkaConsumer\n\nfrom crowdpay_connect.events import deserialise_event  # shared util\n\nlogger = logging.getLogger(__name__)\n\n\nclass CrowdPodSearchProjector:\n    INDEX = \"crowdpod_search_v1\"\n\n    def __init__(self, es: AsyncElasticsearch, bootstrap_servers: str):\n        self._es = es\n        self._consumer = AIOKafkaConsumer(\n            \"crowdpay.events\",\n            bootstrap_servers=bootstrap_servers,\n            group_id=\"crowdpod-search\",\n            value_deserializer=lambda b: json.loads(b.decode()),\n            enable_auto_commit=False,\n        )\n\n    async def run(self) -> None:\n        await self._consumer.start()\n        try:\n            async for msg in self._consumer:\n                evt = deserialise_event(msg.value)\n                await self._handle_event(evt)\n                await self._consumer.commit()\n        except Exception:\n            logger.exception(\"Projection loop crashed!\")\n        finally:\n            await self._consumer.stop()\n\n    # --- Event Handlers ----------------------------------------------------\n\n    async def _handle_event(self, event: Any) -> None:\n        if event[\"event_type\"] == \"CrowdPodCreated\":\n            await self._es.index(\n                index=self.INDEX,\n                id=event[\"pod_id\"],\n                document={\n                    \"name\": event[\"name\"],\n                    \"currency\": event[\"currency\"],\n                    \"owner_id\": event[\"owner_id\"],\n                    \"created_at\": event[\"occurred_at\"],\n                },\n                op_type=\"create\",\n            )\n        elif event[\"event_type\"] == \"FundsDeposited\":\n            await self._es.update(\n                index=self.INDEX,\n                id=event[\"pod_id\"],\n                script={\n                    \"source\": \"ctx._source.balance_minor += params.amt\",\n                    \"params\": {\"amt\": event[\"amount_minor\"]},\n                },\n                retry_on_conflict=5,\n            )\n```\n\nDesign notes:\n\n1. **Exactly-once semantics** are achieved via Kafka offsets + deterministic `id` in ElasticSearch.  \n2. **Backfills**: Replaying the full event history is safe; the script handles duplicate events.\n\n---\n\n## 7. Saga Orchestrator (Settlement Flow)\n\n```python\n\"\"\"\ncrowdpay_connect.saga.settlement\n~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~\nDistributed Saga to convert multi-currency deposits and\nsettle funds to external banking rails.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport asyncio\nimport logging\nimport uuid\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass\nfrom enum import Enum, auto\n\nfrom crowdpay_connect.bus import publish_command, publish_event\n\nlog = logging.getLogger(__name__)\n\n\nclass SagaState(Enum):\n    INITIATED = auto()\n    FX_RESERVED = auto()\n    BANK_RESERVED = auto()\n    COMPLETED = auto()\n    COMPENSATING = auto()\n    FAILED = auto()\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass SagaContext:\n    saga_id: str\n    pod_id: str\n    amount_minor: int\n    src_currency: str\n    dest_currency: str\n    beneficiary_iban: str\n\n\nclass SettlementSaga:\n    TIMEOUT = 30  # seconds for each step\n\n    def __init__(self, ctx: SagaContext) -> None:\n        self.ctx = ctx\n        self.state = SagaState.INITIATED\n\n    async def execute(self) -> None:\n        try:\n            await self._reserve_fx()\n            await self._reserve_bank_slot()\n            await self._finalise()\n        except Exception as exc:\n            log.warning(\"Saga %s failed: %s – compensating …\", self.ctx.saga_id, exc)\n            await self._compensate()\n            self.state = SagaState.FAILED\n            raise\n\n    async def _reserve_fx(self) -> None:\n        publish_command(\n            \"fx.reserve\",\n            saga_id=self.ctx.saga_id,\n            amount=self.ctx.amount_minor,\n            src=self.ctx.src_currency,\n            dest=self.ctx.dest_currency,\n        )\n        await self._await_event(\"fx.reserved\")\n        self.state = SagaState.FX_RESERVED\n\n    async def _reserve_bank_slot(self) -> None:\n        publish_command(\n            \"bank.reserve\",\n            saga_id=self.ctx.saga_id,\n            amount=self.ctx.amount_minor,\n            currency=self.ctx.dest_currency,\n            iban=self.ctx.beneficiary_iban,\n        )\n        await self._await_event(\"bank.slot_reserved\")\n        self.state = SagaState.BANK_RESERVED\n\n    async def _finalise(self) -> None:\n        publish_command(\"bank.settle\", saga_id=self.ctx.saga_id)\n        await self._await_event(\"bank.settled\")\n        publish_event(\"saga.settlement.completed\", saga_id=self.ctx.saga_id)\n        self.state = SagaState.COMPLETED\n\n    async def _compensate(self) -> None:\n        self.state = SagaState.COMPENSATING\n        publish_command(\"fx.cancel\", saga_id=self.ctx.saga_id)\n        publish_command(\"bank.release\", saga_id=self.ctx.saga_id)\n        await asyncio.gather(\n            self._await_event(\"fx.cancelled\"),\n            self._await_event(\"bank.slot_released\"),\n        )\n        publish_event(\"saga.settlement.compensated\", saga_id=self.ctx.saga_id)\n\n    async def _await_event(self, name: str) -> None:\n        # Pseudo-implementation; production code uses Kafka + Correlation-ID\n        try:\n            await asyncio.wait_for(_event_bus_wait(name, self.ctx.saga_id), timeout=self.TIMEOUT)\n        except asyncio.TimeoutError as exc:\n            raise RuntimeError(f\"Timeout waiting for {name}\") from exc\n\n\ndef start_settlement(\n    pod_id: str,\n    amount_minor: int,\n    src_currency: str,\n    dest_currency: str,\n    beneficiary_iban: str,\n) -> str:\n    saga_id = str(uuid.uuid4())\n    ctx = SagaContext(\n        saga_id=saga_id,\n        pod_id=pod_id,\n        amount_minor=amount_minor,\n        src_currency=src_currency,\n        dest_currency=dest_currency,\n        beneficiary_iban=beneficiary_iban,\n    )\n\n    asyncio.create_task(SettlementSaga(ctx).execute())\n    return saga_id\n```\n\n---\n\n## 8. Compliance & Security by Design\n\n1. **Immutability** – No PII lives in the event payload; sensitive data is encrypted via **envelope encryption** before being stored as `event_data`.  \n2. **Field-level encryption** – KMS-backed keys rotate every 90 days.  \n3. **GDPR Right to Erasure** – Events keep a `subject_id` pointer; “deletion” is processed by cryptographically shredding the CEK (Content Encryption Key).  \n4. **Audit trail** – All event append operations store the `meta_data` → `trace_id`, `source_ip`, `kyc_level`, `bank_id`.  \n5. **Tamper detection** – Merkle root per day signed with server HSM.\n\n---\n\n## 9. Operational Playbook\n\n| Action                               | Cmd / Tooling                                       |\n|--------------------------------------|-----------------------------------------------------|\n| Rebuild read models                  | `python -m crowdpay_connect.tools.replay_events`    |\n| Take snapshot for hot restore        | `SELECT crowdpay.snapshot_make('CrowdPod', ...);`   |\n| Verify merkle integrity              | `crowdpay-cli merkle verify --date 2024-05-30`      |\n| Truncate outbox table (processed)    | Schedule: cron daily via `crowdpay_cleaner`         |\n\n---\n\n## 10. Testing Strategy\n\n1. **Property-based tests** (`hypothesis`) for aggregates guarantee invariants (`balance ≥ 0`).  \n2. **Event fixture contracts** verified through **JSON Schema** in CI.  \n3. **Replay test**: every PR triggers a full replay of prod anon-scrubbed events against head commit; projection checksum must match.\n\n---\n\n### Appendix: Event JSON Schemas (OpenAPI 3.1 extract)\n\n```yaml\nCrowdPodCreated:\n  type: object\n  required: [pod_id, owner_id, currency, name, occurred_at]\n  properties:\n    pod_id:      {type: string, format: uuid}\n    owner_id:    {type: string, format: uuid}\n    currency:    {type: string, enum: [USD, EUR, GBP, NGN, GHS]}\n    name:        {type: string, maxLength: 64}\n    occurred_at: {type: string, format: date-time}\n\nFundsDeposited:\n  type: object\n  required: [pod_id, member_id, amount_minor, currency, tx_ref, occurred_at]\n  properties:\n    pod_id:       {type: string, format: uuid}\n    member_id:    {type: string, format: uuid}\n    amount_minor: {type: integer, minimum: 1}\n    currency:     {type: string}\n    tx_ref:       {type: string}\n    occurred_at:  {type: string, format: date-time}\n```\n\n---\n\n_© 2024 CrowdPay Connect Architecture Guild_\n```","numLines":614,"startLine":1,"totalLines":614}}}

    \"\"\"\n        Run the saga and return a final PaymentResult—for API serialization.\n\n        Idempotency is expected to be handled by an upstream layer (API GW +\n        database). This coordinator focuses strictly on happy-path/rollback\n        logic.\n        \"\"\"\n        context = SagaContext(command=command)\n        steps = self._build_steps()\n\n        logger.info(\n            \"Starting PaymentSaga: transaction_id=%s, idempotency_key=%s\",\n            context.transaction_id,\n            command.idempotency_key,\n        )\n\n        completed_steps: List[SagaStep] = []\n\n        try:\n            for step in steps:\n                logger.debug(\"Executing step '%s' for transaction %s\", step.name, context.transaction_id)\n                step.action(context)  # Execute main action\n                completed_steps.append(step)\n                logger.debug(\"Step '%s' completed\", step.name)\n        except Exception as exc:\n            logger.warning(\"Step '%s' failed: %s. )\n                raise\n\n    # --------------------------------------------------------------------- #\n    #                           Saga step actions                           #\n    # self.risk.assess_payment(\n            user_id=ctx.command.payer_id,\n            amount=ctx.command.amount,\n            currency=ctx.command.source_currency,\n        )\n        if not approved:\n )\n\n    def _action_debit_payer(self, ctx: SagaContext) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Debit the payer's wallet.\"\"\"\n        ledger_id = self.wallet.debit(\n            user_id=ctx.command.payer_id,\n            amount=ctx.command.amount,\n            currency=ctx.command.source_currency,\n        )\n        ctx.journal[\"debit_ledger_id\"] = ledger_id\n        ctx.events.append(\n            {\"event_type\": \"WalletDebited\", \"ledger_id\": ledger_id}\n        )\n\n    def _compensate_debit_payer(self, ctx: SagaContext) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Reverse payer debit.\"\"\"\n        ledger_id = self.wallet.credit(\n            user_id=ctx.command.payer_id,\n            amount=ctx.command.amount,\n            currency=ctx.command.source_currency,\n        )\n        ctx.events.append(\n            {\n                \"event_type\": \"WalletDebitReversed\",\n                \"original_ledger_id\": ctx.journal.get(\"debit_ledger_id\"),\n                \"compensation_ledger_id\": ledger_id,\n            }\n        )\n\n    def _action_currency_conversion(self, ctx: SagaContext) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Convert funds if source & target currencies differ.\"\"\"\n        if ctx.command.source_currency == ctx.command.target_currency:\n            ctx.journal[\"converted_amount\"] = ctx.command.amount\n            logger.debug(\"No currency conversion required.\")\n            return\n\n        converted_amount = self.forex.convert(\n            amount=ctx.command.amount,\n            source=ctx.command.source_currency,\n            target=ctx.command.target_currency,\n        )\n        if converted_amount <= 0:\n            raise SagaExecutionError(\"Currency conversion failed\")\n\n        ctx.journal[\"converted_amount\"] = _compensate_currency_conversion(self, ctx: SagaContext) -> None:\n        \"\"\"\n        Currency conversion compensation is effectively a no-op because the\n        actual wallet funds have not left In real-life scenarios, fx\n        markets may require booking a compensating trade.\n        \"\"\"\n        ctx.events.append(\n            {\n                \"event_type\": \"CurrencyConversionVoided\",\n                \"transaction_id\": ctx.transaction_id,\n            }\n        )\n\n    def _action_credit_crowdpod(self, ctx: SagaContext) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Credit funds to the CrowdPod wallet.\"\"\"\n        amount = ctx.journal.get(\"converted_amount\", ctx.command.amount)\n        ledger_id = self.wallet.credit(\n            user_id=ctx.command.crowdpod_id,\n            amount=amount,\n            currency=ctx.command.target_currency,\n        )\n        ctx.journal[\"credit_ledger_id\"] = ledger_id\n        ctx.events.append(\n            {\"event_type\": \"CrowdPodCredited\", \"ledger_id\": ledger_id}\n        )\n\n    def _compensate_credit_crowdpod(self, ctx: SagaContext) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Reverse CrowdPod credit.\"\"\"\n        amount = ctx.journal.get(\"converted_amount\", ctx.command.amount)\n        ledger_id = self.wallet.debit(\n            user_id=ctx.command.crowdpod_id,\n            amount=amount,\n            currency=ctx.command.target_currency,\n        )\n        ctx.events.append(\n            {\n                \"event_type\": \"CrowdPodCreditReversed\",\n                \"original_ledger_id\": ctx.journal.get(\"credit_ledger_id\"),\n                \"compensation_ledger_id\": ledger_id,\n            }\n        )\n\n    def _action_settlement(self, ctx: SagaContext) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Mark transaction as settled in the clearing subsystem.\"\"\"\n        settled = self.settlement.settle_transaction(ctx.transaction_id)\n        if not settled:\n            raise SagaExecutionError(\"Settlement failed\")\n        ctx.events.append(\n            {\"event_type\": \"TransactionSettled\", \"transaction_id\": ctx.transaction_id}\n        )\n\n    def _compensate_settlement(self, ctx: SagaContext) -> None:\n        \"\"\"\n        Settlement compensation is domain-specific; often done via a manual\n        “break” queue. Here we merely publish an alert.\n        \"\"\"\n        self.bus.publish(\n            topic=\"payments.lifecycle\",\n            event={\n                \"event_type\": \"SettlementVoided\",\n                \"transaction_id\": ctx.transaction_id,\n            },\n        )\n        ctx.events.append(\n            {\"event_type\": \"SettlementVoided\", \"transaction_id\": ctx.transaction_id}\n        )\n```","numLines":453,"startLine":1,"totalLines":453}}}

window.\"\"\"\n\n\n# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# In-memory repository for demonstration purposes\n# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n\nclass SagaRepository:\n    \"\"\"\n    In production this would be backed by PostgreSQL, Redis, or a document store.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self) -> None:\n        self._store: Dict[str, Saga] = {}\n\n    async def save(self, saga: Saga) -> None:\n        saga.updated_at = datetime.utcnow()\n        self._store[saga.saga_id] = saga\n        logger.debug(\"Saga %s persisted with status %s\", saga.saga_id, saga.status)\n\n    async def get(self, saga_id: str) -> Optional[Saga]:\n        return self._store.get(saga_id)\n\n    async def delete(self, saga_id: str) -> None:\n        self._store.pop(saga_id, None)\n\n\n# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Coordinator implementation\n# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n\nclass SagaCoordinator:\n    \"\"\"\n    Orchestrates multi-step payment Sagas for CrowdPay Connect.\n\n    Example\n    -------\n    >>> bus = EventBus()\n    >>> repo = SagaRepository()\n    >>> coordinator = SagaCoordinator(bus, repo)\n    >>> saga = coordinator.create_saga(\n    ...     steps=[\n    ...         SagaStep(\"debit\", debit_wallet, credit_wallet),\n    ...         SagaStep(\"credit\", credit_recipient, debit_recipient),\n    ...     ]\n    ... )\n    >>> await coordinator.execute(saga.saga_id)\n    \"\"\"\n\n    BUS_TOPIC_SAGA_UPDATED = \"saga.updated\"\n\n    def __init__(self, bus: EventBus, repository: SagaRepository) -> None:\n        self.bus = bus\n        self.repository = repository\n\n    # --------------------------------------------------------------------- #\n    # Public API\n    # --------------------------------------------------------------------- #\n\n    def create_saga(self, steps: Iterable[SagaStep], ttl_minutes: int = 5) -> Saga:\n        saga_id = str(uuid.uuid4())\n        saga = Saga(\n            saga_id=saga_id,\n            steps=list(steps),\n            ttl=timedelta(minutes=ttl_minutes),\n        )\n        # Persist synchronously to guarantee idempotent starts\n        asyncio.get_event_loop().run_until_complete(self.repository.save(saga))\n        logger.info(\"Created new Saga: %s\", saga_id)\n        return saga\n\n    async def execute(self, saga_id: str) -> Saga:\n        saga = await self._load_saga_or_raise(saga_id)\n        if saga.status not in {SagaStatus.PENDING, SagaStatus.IN_PROGRESS}:\n            logger.warning(\"Saga %s already completed with status %s\", saga_id, saga.status)\n            return saga\n\n        saga.status = SagaStatus.IN_PROGRESS\n        await self.repository.save(saga)\n        await self._publish_update(saga)\n\n        try:\n            for step in saga.steps:\n                if saga.is_expired():\n                    raise SagaTimeoutError(f\"Saga {saga.saga_id} timed-out\")\n\n                if step.status is StepStatus.SUCCESS:\n                    # Allows Saga resumption after crash\n                    logger.debug(\"Skipping already successful step: %s\", step.name)\n                    continue\n\n                await step.execute()\n                await self.repository.save(saga)\n                await self._publish_update(saga)\n\n            saga.status = SagaStatus.COMPLETED\n            await self.repository.save(saga)\n            await self._publish_update(saga)\n            logger.info(\"Saga %s completed successfully\", saga.saga_id)\n            return saga\n\n        except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001\n            logger.error(\"Saga %s failed due to %s. Triggering compensation.\", saga.saga_id, exc)\n            saga.status = SagaStatus.FAILED\n            await self.repository.save(saga)\n            await self._publish_update(saga)\n\n            # Attempt compensations in **reverse** order\n            await self._run_compensations(saga)\n            return saga\n\n    # --------------------------------------------------------------------- #\n    # Internals\n    # --------------------------------------------------------------------- #\n\n    async def _run_compensations(self, saga: Saga) -> None:\n        for step in reversed(saga.steps):\n            if step.status is StepStatus.SUCCESS:\n                try:\n                    await step.compensate()\n                except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001\n                    # compensation failure is fatal — escalate & stop loop\n                    saga.status = SagaStatus.COMPENSATED\n                    await self.repository.save(saga)\n                    await self._publish_update(saga)\n                    raise\n        saga.status = SagaStatus.COMPENSATED\n        await self.repository.save(saga)\n        await self._publish_update(saga)\n        logger.info(\"Saga %s compensated successfully\", saga.saga_id)\n\n    async def _publish_update(self, saga: Saga) -> None:\n        event = Event(\n            topic=self.BUS_TOPIC_SAGA_UPDATED,\n            payload=saga.to_dict(),\n        )\n        await self.bus.publish(event)\n\n    async def _load_saga_or_raise(self, saga_id: str) -> Saga:\n        saga = await self.repository.get(saga_id)\n        if not saga:\n            raise ValueError(f\"Saga {saga_id} not found\")\n        return saga\n\n\n# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Example step actions (place-holders)\n# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\nasync def debit_wallet() -> None:\n    \"\"\"\n    Placeholder for wallet debit service call.\n    \"\"\"\n    await asyncio.sleep(0.1)  # simulate IO delay\n    logger.debug(\"Debited wallet successfully\")\n\n\nasync def credit_wallet() -> None:\n    \"\"\"\n    Compensation for debit_wallet().\n    \"\"\"\n    await asyncio.sleep(0.1)\n    logger.debug(\"Credited wallet rollback successfully\")\n\n\nasync def credit_recipient() -> None:\n    await asyncio.sleep(0.1)\n    logger.debug(\"Credited recipient successfully\")\n\n\nasync def debit_recipient() -> None:\n    await asyncio.sleep(0.1)\n    logger.debug(\"Debited recipient compensation successfully\")\n\n\n# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Module test (only run when executed directly)\n# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":  # pragma: no cover\n    async def _demo() -> None:\n        # Configure root logger for demo purposes\n        logging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO, format=\"%(levelname)s %(message)s\")\n\n        bus = EventBus()\n        repo = SagaRepository()\n        coordinator = SagaCoordinator(bus, repo)\n\n        # Subscribe to Saga updates\n        bus.subscribe(\n            SagaCoordinator.BUS_TOPIC_SAGA_UPDATED,\n            lambda e: logger.info(\"EVENT: %s\", e.to_json()),\n        )\n\n        # Build a saga with two steps\n        saga = coordinator.create_saga(\n            steps=[\n                SagaStep(\n                    name=\"debit_payer\",\n                    action=debit_wallet,\n                    compensation=credit_wallet,\n                ),\n                SagaStep(\n                    name=\"credit_receiver\",\n                    action=credit_recipient,\n                    compensation=debit_recipient,\n                ),\n            ]\n        )\n\n        # Execute saga\n        await coordinator.execute(saga.saga_id)\n\n    asyncio.run(_demo())\n```","numLines":403,"startLine":1,"totalLines":403}}}

Arbitrary contextual data shared across steps\n    data: Dict[str, Any] = Column(_JSONEncodedDict, default=dict, nullable=False)\n\n    # Captures unrecoverable error messages (if any)\n    error_message: Optional[str] = Column(Text, nullable=True)\n\n    created_at: datetime = Column(\n        DateTime(timezone=True),\n        nullable=False,\n        default=lambda: datetime.now(tz=timezone.utc),\n        index=True,\n    )\n    updated_at: datetime = Column(\n        DateTime(timezone=True),\n        nullable=False,\n        default=lambda: datetime.now(tz=timezone.utc),\n        onupdate=lambda: datetime.now(tz=timezone.utc),\n    )\n\n    # Optimistic-locking version column\n    version: int = Column(Integer, nullable=False, default=1)\n\n    # --------------------------------------------------------------------- #\n    # Factory helpers\n    # --------------------------------------------------------------------- #\n\n    @classmethod\n    def bootstrap(\n        cls,\n        *,\n        saga_name: str,\n        correlation_id: uuid.UUID | None = None,\n        total_steps: int,\n        initial_data: Dict[str, Any] | None = None,\n    ) -> \"SagaState\":\n        \"\"\"\n        Instantiate a new saga record in NOT_STARTED phase.\n        \"\"\"\n        correlation_id = correlation_id or uuid.uuid4()\n        logger.debug(\n            \"Bootstrapping new SagaState '%s' (correlation_id=%s)\",\n            saga_name,\n            correlation_id,\n        )\n        return cls(\n            saga_name=saga_name,\n            correlation_id=correlation_id,\n            total_steps=total_steps,\n            data=initial_data or {},\n        )\n\n    # --------------------------------------------------------------------- #\n    # Business-logic manipulation helpers\n    # --------------------------------------------------------------------- #\n\n    def start(self) -> None:\n        \"\"\"\n        Transition the saga into IN_PROGRESS, if possible.\n        \"\"\"\n        self._ensure_phase(expected=SagaPhase.NOT_STARTED)\n        self.phase = SagaPhase.IN_PROGRESS\n        self.current_step = 1\n        logger.info(\"Saga '%s' started (step %s/%s)\", self.id, self.current_step, self.total_steps)\n\n    def advance_step(self, data_patch: Dict[str, Any] | None = None) -> None:\n        \"\"\"\n        Marks the current step as completed and advances to the next one.\n        Mutates optional internal saga data using the provided patch.\n        \"\"\"\n        self._ensure_phase(expected=SagaPhase.IN_PROGRESS)\n        if self.current_step >= self.total_steps:\n            raise ValueError(\n                f\"Saga '{self.id}' is already on the last step ({self.current_step}).\",\n            )\n        self.current_step += 1\n        logger.debug(\"Saga '%s' advanced to step %s\", self.id, self.current_step)\n        if data_patch:\n            logger.debug(\"Patching Saga data with %s\", data_patch)\n            self.data.update(data_patch)\n\n        if self.current_step == self.total_steps:\n            self.complete()\n\n    def complete(self) -> None:\n        \"\"\"\n        Finish the saga successfully.\n        \"\"\"\n        self._ensure_phase(expected=SagaPhase.IN_PROGRESS)\n        self.phase = SagaPhase.COMPLETED\n        logger.info(\"Saga '%s' completed successfully.\", self.id)\n\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ #\n    # Failure handling\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ #\n\n    def fail(self, reason: str, compensate: bool = False) -> None:\n        \"\"\"\n        Registers a failure.  If `compensate=True` the saga enters COMPENSATING\n        and will later be rolled back; otherwise it's FAILED (terminal).\n        \"\"\"\n        if self.phase.is_terminal:\n            logger.warning(\n                \"Attempted to fail saga '%s' but it is already terminal (%s)\",\n                self.id,\n                self.phase,\n            )\n            return\n\n        self.error_message = reason\n        self.phase = SagaPhase.COMPENSATING if compensate else SagaPhase.FAILED\n        logger.error(\n            \"Saga '%s' transitioned to %s due to: %s\",\n            self.id,\n            self.phase.value,\n            reason,\n        )\n\n    def rollback(self) -> None:\n        \"\"\"\n        Mark the saga as fully rolled back after successful compensation tasks.\n        \"\"\"\n        self._ensure_phase(expected=SagaPhase.COMPENSATING)\n        self.phase = SagaPhase.ROLLED_BACK\n        logger.info(\"Saga '%s' has been rolled back.\", self.id)\n\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ #\n    # Internal helpers\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ #\n\n    def _ensure_phase(self, *, expected: SagaPhase) -> None:\n        \"\"\"\n        Raises a ValueError if the saga isn't currently in the expected phase.\n        \"\"\"\n        if self.phase != expected:\n            raise ValueError(\n                f\"Saga '{self.id}' must be in phase {expected}, \"\n                f\"but is {self.phase}.\",\n            )\n\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ #\n    # Optimistic locking / versioning\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ #\n\n    def increment_version(self) -> None:\n        \"\"\"\n        Increments the version number prior to flushing to the DB, enabling\n        optimistic concurrency control.\n        \"\"\"\n        logger.debug(\n            \"Incrementing version of Saga '%s' from %s to %s\",\n            self.id,\n            self.version,\n            self.version + 1,\n        )\n        self.version += 1\n\n\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------- #\n# SQLAlchemy event listeners\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------- #\n\n@event.listens_for(SagaState, \"before_update\", propagate=True)\ndef _receive_before_update(mapper, connection, target: SagaState):  # noqa: D401\n    \"\"\"\n    SQLAlchemy event hook that fires before an UPDATE statement.  It validates\n    the version column to implement application-level optimistic locking and\n    bumps the version number afterwards.\n    \"\"\"\n    # Retrieve current version persisted in the database\n    table = SagaState.__table__\n    current_version: int | None = connection.execute(\n        table.select()\n        .with_only_columns(table.c.version)\n        .where(table.c.id == target.id)\n    ).scalar()\n\n    if current_version is None:\n        raise StaleDataError(\n            f\"Saga '{target.id}' disappeared from the database whilst updating.\",\n        )\n\n    if current_version != target.version:\n        raise OptimisticLockError(\n            f\"Saga '{target.id}' has been modified concurrently \"\n            f\"(db_version={current_version}, entity_version={target.version}).\",\n        )\n\n    # All good – increment the in-memory version so it gets stored by UPDATE\n    target.increment_version()\n```","numLines":319,"startLine":1,"totalLines":319}}}

Commit entries.\n        5. Publish events.\n\n        Returns\n        -------\n        Tuple[LedgerEntry, LedgerEntry]\n            `(debit_entry, credit_entry)`\n        \"\"\"\n        if await self._repo.fetch_entry_by_external_id(external_txn_id):\n            logger.info(\"Duplicate external_txn_id=%s.        return balances\n\n        async def _convert(m: Money) -> Money:\n            if m.currency == in_currency:\n                return m\n            return await self._fx.convert(m, in_currency)\n\n        tasks = [asyncio.create_task(_convert(m)) for m in balances.values()]\n        converted = await asyncio.gather(*tasks)\n        return {in_currency: sum((m.amount for m in converted), Decimal(\"0\"))}\n\n    # --------------------------------------------------------------------- #\n    # Internal helpers                                                      #\n    # --------------------------------------------------------------------- #\n\n    async def _assert_sufficient_balance(\n        self, wallet_id: uuid.UUID, debit_amount: Money\n    ) -> None:\n        balances = await self._repo.list_wallet_balance(wallet_id)\n        available = balances.get(debit_amount.currency, Money(Decimal(\"0\"), debit_amount.currency))\n        if available.amount < debit_amount.amount:\n            raise InsufficientFundsError(\n                f\"Wallet {wallet_id} has {available.amount}, \"\n                f\"needs {debit_amount.amount} available.amount,\n            debit_amount.amount,\n        )\n\n\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #\n# Saga Steps                                                                  #\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #\n\n\nclass _PersistEntriesStep:\n    def __init__(self, repo: AbstractLedgerRepository, *entries: LedgerEntry):\n        self._repo = repo\n        self._entries = entries\n\n    async def invoke(self) -> None:\n        async with self._repo.transaction():\n            await self._repo.save_entries(self._entries)\n        logger.debug(\"Ledger entries persisted: %s\", [e.id for e in self._entries])\n\n    async def compensate(self) -> None:\n        async with self._repo.transaction():\n            await self._repo.mark_reversed(\n                [e.id for e in self._entries],\n                reason=\"Saga compensation before commit\",\n                failed=True,\n            )\n        logger.debug(\"Persist saga compensation applied to entries: %s\", [e.id for e in self._entries])\n\n\nclass _CommitEntriesStep:\n    def __init__(self, repo: AbstractLedgerRepository, *entries: LedgerEntry):\n        self._repo = repo\n        self._entries = entries\n\n    async def invoke(self) -> None:\n        # Local in-memory state transition\n        for e in self._entries:\n            e.commit()\n        async with self._repo.transaction():\n            await self._repo.mark_committed([e.id for e in self._entries])\n        logger.debug(\"Ledger entries committed: %s\", [e.id for e in self._entries])\n\n    async def compensate(self) -> None:\n        async with self._repo.transaction():\n            await self._repo.mark_reversed(\n                [e.id for e in self._entries],\n                reason=\"Saga compensation post commit\",\n                failed=True,\n            )\n        logger.debug(\"Commit saga compensation executed for entries: %s\", [e.id for e in self._entries])\n\n\nclass _PublishEventsStep:\n    \"\"\"\n    Final saga step that broadcasts domain events. This step is intentionally\n    placed last so that consumers only see COMMITTED entries.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, publisher: AbstractEventPublisher, *entries: LedgerEntry):\n        self._publisher = publisher\n        self._entries = entries\n        self._published: bool = False\n\n    async def invoke(self) -> None:\n        tasks = [\n            self._publisher.publish(\n                \"ledger.entry.committed\",\n                {\n                    \"entry_id\": str(e.id),\n                    \"wallet_id\": str(e.wallet_id),\n                    \"type\": e.entry_type,\n                    \"amount\": str(e.money.amount),\n                    \"currency\": e.money.currency,\n                    \"external_txn_id\": e.external_txn_id,\n                    \"timestamp\": e.created_at.isoformat(),\n                },\n            )\n            for e in self._entries\n        ]\n        await asyncio.gather(*tasks)\n        self._published = True\n        logger.debug(\"Ledger events published for entries: %s\", [e.id for e in self._entries])\n\n    async def compensate(self) -> None:\n        if not self._published:\n            return\n        # Events are immutable; to compensate we emit a \"reversed\" event\n        tasks = [\n            self._publisher.publish(\n                \"ledger.entry.reversed\",\n                {\"entry_id\": str(e.id), \"reason\": \"Saga compensation\"},\n            )\n            for e in self._entries\n        ]\n        await asyncio.gather(*tasks)\n        logger.debug(\"Compensation events emitted for entries: %s\", [e.id for e in self._entries])\n```","numLines":511,"startLine":1,"totalLines":511}}}

The default runtime uses **aiokafka**, but swapping to\nRabbitMQ (via **aio-pika**) or AWS MSK/IAM is trivial — simply implement\n`AbstractBrokerConsumer` and plug it into `EventConsumer`.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport asyncio\nimport json\nimport logging\nimport os\nimport signal\nimport sys\nimport time\nfrom abc import ABC, abstractmethod\nfrom contextlib import asynccontextmanager\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass\nfrom typing import Any, Awaitable, Callable, Dict, Final, List, Mapping, Type\n\nimport aiokafka\nfrom aiokafka import AIOKafkaConsumer, ConsumerRecord\nfrom pydantic import BaseModel, ValidationError\nfrom tenacity import retry, retry_if_exception_type, stop_after_attempt, wait_exponential\n\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #\n# Configuration & Constants\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #\n\n_LOGGER: Final = logging.getLogger(\"crowdpay.wallet.events.consumer\")\n\nKAFKA_BOOTSTRAP: Final[str] = os.getenv(\"KAFKA_BOOTSTRAP_SERVERS\", \"localhost:9092\")\nWALLET_EVENTS_TOPIC: Final[str] = os.getenv(\"WALLET_EVENTS_TOPIC\", \"wallet.events\")\nSYSTEM_EVENTS_TOPIC: Final[str] = os.getenv(\"SYSTEM_EVENTS_TOPIC\", \"system.events\")\nCONSUMER_GROUP: Final[str] = os.getenv(\"WALLET_CONSUMER_GROUP\", \"wallet_service\")\n\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #\n# Event Model\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #\n\n\nclass Event(BaseModel):\n    \"\"\"\n    Base CloudEvent-ish model.\n\n    All events MUST include at least the following metadata contract:\n    \"\"\"\n\n    id: str\n    source: str\n    type: str\n    spec_version: str = \"1.0\"\n    data: Dict[str, Any]\n    subject: str | None = None\n    time: float | None = None  # Epoch (ms)\n\n\n# Wallet domain-specific events ------------------------------------------------\n\n\nclass WalletCreated(Event):\n    type: str = \"wallet.created\"\n    data: Dict[str, Any]  # { \"wallet_id\": str, \"owner_id\": str, \"currency\": str, ... }\n\n\nclass FundsDeposited(Event):\n    type: str = \"wallet.funds_deposited\"\n    data: Dict[str, Any]  # TODO: Persist wallet aggregate / outbox integration event\n    # await wallet_repository.create_wallet(...)\n    # await outbox.publish_async(\"wallet.read_model.updated\", ...)\n\n\nasync def handle_funds_deposited(event: FundsDeposited) -> None:\n    _LOGGER.info(\"💰 Funds deposited ‑ wallet=%s amount=%s%s\",\n                 event.data[\"wallet_id\"], event.data[\"amount\"], event.data[\"currency\"])\n    # TODO: Balance validations / AML scanning / ledger postings\n\n\nasync def handle_transfer_initiated(event: TransferInitiated) -> None:\n    _LOGGER.info(\"🔄 Transfer initiated ‑ ref=%s\", event.data.get(\"transfer_ref\"))\n    # TODO: Saga orchestrator handshake\n\n\nasync def handle_transfer_completed(event: TransferCompleted) -> None:\n    _LOGGER.info(\"✅ Transfer completed ‑ ref=%s\", event.data.get(\"transfer_ref\"))\n    # TODO: Finalise ledger / notify parties\n\n\nasync def handle_transfer_failed(event: TransferFailed) -> None:\n    _LOGGER.warning(\"❌ Transfer failed ‑ ref=%s reason=%s\",\n                    event.data.get(\"transfer_ref\"), event.data.get(\"reason\"))\n\n\n# Map event *classes* to concrete async handlers\n_EVENT_HANDLER_MAP: Dict[Type[Event], Callable[[Event], Awaitable[None]]] = {\n    WalletCreated: handle_wallet_created,\n    FundsDeposited: handle_funds_deposited,\n    FundsWithdrawn: handle_funds_withdrawn,\n    TransferInitiated: handle_transfer_initiated,\n    TransferCompleted: handle_transfer_completed,\n    TransferFailed: handle_transfer_failed,\n}\n\n\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #\n# Broker Abstraction\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #\n\nclass AbstractBrokerConsumer(ABC):\n    \"\"\"Strategy interface to allow pluggable broker implementations.\"\"\"\n\n    @abstractmethod\n    async def __aenter__(self) -> \"AbstractBrokerConsumer\":  # We purposefully block with an\n        infinite timeout to maximise throughput (co-operative cancellation\n        handled by enclosing task).\n        \"\"\"\n        try:\n            record: StopAsyncIteration\n        except Exception:\n            _LOGGER.exception(\"Fatal error during Kafka polling loop.\")\n            await asyncio.sleep(1)\n            raise\n\n    async def commit(self) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Commit offsets for all buffered records (if any).\"\"\"\n        if not self._records:\n            return\n        await self._consumer.commit()\n --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #\n\n@dataclass(slots=True)\nclass ProcessingContext:\n    \"\"\"Runtime context used during event processing.\"\"\"\n (from `uvicorn` or dedicated entrypoint):\n\n        asyncio.run(EventConsumer().run_forever())\n\n    The **run_forever** coroutine registers a clean shutdown handler (SIGINT /\n    SIGTERM) and blocks the main thread until cancellation.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(\n        self,\n        broker_consumer: AbstractBrokerConsumer | None = None,\n        *,\n        loop: topics=[WALLET_EVENTS_TOPIC, SYSTEM_EVENTS_TOPIC],\n            bootstrap_servers=KAFKA_BOOTSTRAP,\n            group_id=CONSUMER_GROUP,\n            loop=self._loop,\n        )\n        self._shutdown_event: asyncio.Event = asyncio.Event()\n\n    # --------------------------------------------------------------------- #\n    # Public API\n    # --------------------------------------------------------------------- #\n\n    async def run_forever(self) -> None:\n        self._register_signal_handlers()\n        async with self._broker_consumer:\n            _LOGGER.info(\"WalletService EventConsumer started — awaiting messages…\")\n            while not self._shutdown_event.is_set():\n                try:\n                    topic, raw = await self._broker_consumer.__anext__()\n                    ctx = ProcessingContext(topic=topic, raw_payload=raw)\n                    await self._process_with_retry(ctx)\n                    await self._broker_consumer.commit()\n                except StopAsyncIteration:\n                    break\n                except asyncio.CancelledError:\n                    break\n                except Exception:\n                    _LOGGER.exception(\"Unhandled exception escaped main loop — continuing.\")\n                    # Sleep briefly to avoid tight loop cascade\n                    await asyncio.sleep(1)\n        _LOGGER.info(\"EventConsumer shutdown complete.\")\n\n    # --------------------------------------------------------------------- #\n    # Internals\n    # --------------------------------------------------------------------- #\n\n    @retry(\n        retry=retry_if_exception_type(Exception),\n        wait=wait_exponential(multiplier=1, min=2, max=30),\n        stop=stop_after_attempt(5),\n        reraise=True,\n    )\n    async def _process_with_retry(self, ctx: ProcessingContext) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Process an event with exponential back-off retries.\"\"\"\n        ctx.attempts += 1\n        try:\n            event = self._deserialize(ctx.raw_payload)\n            await self._dispatch(event)\n        except ValidationError as ve:\n            _LOGGER.error(\"Deserialisation error — dropping event (payload=%s) err=%s\",\n                          ctx.raw_payload, ve)\n        except Exception:\n            _LOGGER.exception(\"Processing json.loads(raw.decode(\"utf8\"))\n        except json.JSONDecodeError as err:\n            _LOGGER.error(\"JSON decode error: %s\", err)\n            raise\n\n        evt_type: str | None = payload.get(\"type\")\n        model_cls = _EVENT_REGISTRY.get(evt_type, Event)  # Fallback to generic\n\n        return model_cls.parse_obj(payload)\n\n    async def _dispatch(self, event: Event) -> None:\n        handler = _EVENT_HANDLER_MAP.get(type(event))\n        if not handler:\n            _LOGGER.debug(\"No handler registered for event type=%s — ignoring.\", event.type)\n            return\n        _LOGGER.debug(\"Dispatching event id=%s type=%s\", event.id, event.type)\n        await handler(event)\n\n    # --------------------------------------------------------------------- #\n    # Graceful shutdown helpers\n    # --------------------------------------------------------------------- #\n\n    def _register_signal_handlers(self) -> None:\n        try:\n            for sig in (signal.SIGINT, signal.SIGTERM):\n                self._loop.add_signal_handler(sig, lambda s=sig: asyncio.create_task(self._shutdown(s)))\n        except NotImplementedError:\n            # Signals are not available on Windows (depending on interpreter)\n            _LOGGER.warning(\"Signal handlers not available — relying on other shutdown hooks.\")\n\n    async def _shutdown(self, sig: signal.Signals) -> None:  # noqa: D401\n        _LOGGER.info(\"Received signal %s — initiating shutdown.\", sig.name)\n        self._shutdown_event.set()\n        # Give consumer some time to flush\n        await asyncio.sleep(0.5)\n        await self._broker_consumer.stop()\n\n\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #\n# Entrypoint (python -m crowdpay_connect.services.wallet_service.app.events.consumer)\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #\n\ndef _configure_logging() -> None:\n Locally scoped import to avoid dependency if not needed.\n\n        for src in sources:\n            try:\n                if isinstance(src, pathlib.Path):\n                    if not src.exists():\n                        raise FileNotFoundError(src)\n                    with src.open(\"rt\", encoding=\"utf-8\") as fp:\n                        if src.suffix.lower() in {\".yaml\", \".yml\"}:\n                            raw_rules = yaml.safe_load(fp)\n                        elif src.suffix.lower() == \".json\":\n                            raw_rules = json.load(fp)\n                        else:\n                            LOGGER.warning(\"Unsupported rule file type: %s\", src)\n                            continue\n                elif isinstance(src, dict):\n                    raw_rules = [src]  # Single rule dict\n                else:\n                    LOGGER.warning(\"Unknown rule source: %s\", src)\n                    continue\n\n                for raw in raw_rules:\n                    rule = self._build_rule(raw)\n                    self._rules.append(rule)\n                    LOGGER.debug(\"Loaded rule: %s\", rule.name)\n\n            except Exception as exc:  # pylint: disable=broad-except\n                LOGGER.error(\"Failed to load rule from %s: %s\", src, exc, exc_info=True)\n\n        if not self._rules:\n            raise RuntimeError(\"No rules loaded – risk compliance engine cannot start!\")\n\n    @staticmethod\n    def _build_rule(data: Dict[str, Any]) -> Rule:\n        \"\"\"Validate and convert raw dict to Rule dataclass.\"\"\"\n        required_fields = {\"name\", \"description\", \"conditions\"}\n        missing = required_fields - data.keys()\n        if missing:\n            raise ValueError(f\"Rule definition missing fields: {missing}\")\n\n        return Rule(\n            name=data[\"name\"],\n            description=data[\"description\"],\n            priority=int(data.get(\"priority\", 50)),\n            conditions=tuple(map(str.strip, data[\"conditions\"])),\n            on_match=dict(data.get(\"actions\") or {}),\n            enabled=bool(data.get(\"enabled\", True)),\n        )\n\n\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #\n#  Example usage (will be stripped during package build)                      #\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":  # pragma: no cover\n    logging.basicConfig(level=logging.DEBUG)\n\n    # Example rule definitions defined inline for demonstration purposes.\n    RULES = [\n        {\n            \"name\": \"HighAmountCrossBorder\",\n            \"description\": \"Flag cross-border transfers over $10k\",\n            \"priority\": 90,\n            \"conditions\": [\n                \"transaction.amount > 10_000\",\n                \"transaction.country != user.country\",\n            ],\n            \"actions\": {\n                \"risk_level\": \"HIGH\",\n                \"decision\": \"MANUAL_REVIEW\",\n                \"alert_code\": \"AML-HACB-001\",\n            },\n        },\n        {\n            \"name\": \"UnverifiedUserLargeTx\",\n            \"description\": \"Reject large transactions from non-KYC verified users\",\n            \"priority\": 95,\n            \"conditions\": [\n                \"user.kyc_status != 'VERIFIED'\",\n                \"transaction.amount >= 5_000\",\n            ],\n            \"actions\": {\n                \"risk_level\": \"CRITICAL\",\n                \"decision\": \"REJECT\",\n                \"alert_code\": \"KYC-UVU-002\",\n            },\n        },\n    ]\n\n    engine = RuleEngine(rule_sources=[RULES[0], RULES[1]])\n\n    result = engine.evaluate(\n        tx=Transaction(\n            tx_id=\"TX123456\",\n            pod_id=\"POD987\",\n            user_id=\"USER42\",\n            amount=12_500,\n            currency=\"USD\",\n            country=\"US\",\n            created_at=time(),\n        ),\n        user=UserProfile(\n            user_id=\"USER42\",\n            kyc_status=\"PENDING\",\n            reputation_score=42.0,\n            country=\"CA\",\n            is_pep=False,\n        ),\n    )\n\n    for r in result:\n        print(r)\n```","numLines":467,"startLine":1,"totalLines":467}}}

      logger.debug(\n            \"Calculated risk score %.4f (base=%.4f currency_mod=%.4f rep_mod=%.4f)\",\n            score,\n            base,\n            currency_modifier,\n            reputation_modifier,\n        )\n        return score\n\n    def decide(self, score: float) -> AssessmentDecision:\n        if score < 0.5:\n            return AssessmentDecision.APPROVED\n        if score < 0.75:\n            return AssessmentDecision.HOLD\n        return AssessmentDecision.DECLINED\n\n\n# Global singleton fallbacks – these would be wired using FastAPI dependencies in real\n# codebase, but initialised here for brevity.\n_repository = _InMemoryAssessmentRepository()\n_bus = _NoOpMessageBus()\n_engine = _RiskEngine()\n\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nrouter = APIRouter(prefix=\"/api/v1/assessment\", tags=[\"Risk & Compliance\"])\n\n\ndef get_repository() -> _InMemoryAssessmentRepository:\n    return _repository\n\n\ndef get_bus() -> _NoOpMessageBus:\n    return _bus\n\n\ndef get_risk_engine() -> _RiskEngine:\n    return _engine\n\n\n# persists it as *pending*, triggers asynchronous\n    evaluation in a background task, and returns immediately. Consumers can poll\n    `GET /assessment/{assessment_id}` for final decision.\n    \"\"\"\n    assessment_id = uuid.uuid4()\n    assessment = RiskAssessment(\n        assessment_id=assessment_id,\n        request_ts=datetime.now(tz=timezone.utc),\n        status=AssessmentStatus.PENDING,\n        completed_ts=None,\n        decision=None,\n        score=None,\n        reference_id=payload.reference_id,\n    )\n    repo.add(assessment)\n\n    background_tasks.add_task(\n        _run_risk_assessment_task,\n        assessment=assessment,\n        payload=payload,\n        repo=repo,\n        bus=bus,\n        engine=engine,\n    )\n\n    return AssessmentOut.parse_obj(assessment.dict())\n\n\n@router.get(\n    \"/{assessment_id}\",\n    response_model=AssessmentOut,\n    summary=\"Fetch the latest risk assessment state\",\n)\ndef read_assessment(\n    assessment_id: uuid.UUID,\n    repo: _InMemoryAssessmentRepository = Depends(get_repository),\n) -> AssessmentOut:\n    assessment = repo.get(assessment_id)\n    if not assessment:\n        raise HTTPException(\n            status_code=status.HTTP_404_NOT_FOUND,\n            detail=\"Assessment not found\",\n        )\n    return AssessmentOut.parse_obj(assessment.dict())\n\n\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Background job\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n\ndef _run_risk_assessment_task(\n    assessment: RiskAssessment,\n    payload: AssessmentIn,\n    repo: _InMemoryAssessmentRepository,\n    bus: _NoOpMessageBus,\n    engine: _RiskEngine,\n) -> None:\n    \"\"\"\n    Performs potentially expensive / IO heavy risk scoring logic. Executed by\n    FastAPI's `BackgroundTasks`, Celery worker, or any async task execution pool.\n    \"\"\"\n    logger.info(\"Starting risk evaluation for %s\", assessment.assessment_id)\n\n    try:\n        # ------------------------------------------------------------\n        # Core risk scoring\n        # ------------------------------------------------------------\n        score = engine.score(\n            amount=float(payload.amount),\n            currency_code=payload.currency_code,\n            user_reputation=user_reputation,\n            pod_reputation=pod_reputation,\n        )\n        decision = engine.decide(score)\n\n        # ------------------------------------------------------------\n        # Persist outcome\n        # ------------------------------------------------------------\n        assessment.completed_ts = datetime.now(tz=timezone.utc)\n        assessment.status = AssessmentStatus.COMPLETED\n        assessment.decision = decision\n        assessment.score = score\n        repo.update(assessment)\n\n        # Returns 1.0 for maximum trust.\n    \"\"\"\n    logger.debug(\"Fetching reputation for user %s\", user_id)\n    # The following heuristic is purely illustrative.\n    return 0.9 if int(user_id[-1], 36) % 2 == 0 else 0.7\n\n\ndef _fetch_pod_reputation(pod_id: str) -> float:\n    \"\"\"\n    Placeholder for CrowdPod trust score (derived from up-votes, audit logs, etc.)\n    \"\"\"\n    logger.debug(\"Fetching reputation for pod %s\", pod_id)\n    return 0.95 if int(pod_id[-1], 36) % 3 == 0 else 0.6\n```","numLines":385,"startLine":1,"totalLines":385}}}

Responsibilities\n    13→\n    14→1. **Command Side** – accepts _Transfer_, _Top-Up_, _Withdrawal_ or _FX Swap_\n    15→   commands, validates them against:\n    16→   • risk‐engine, • KYC state, • account limits, • FX quotation TTL.\n    17→2. **Event Store** – append-only stream (`postgresql` → `wal2json` logical\n    18→   slot) storing immutable `TransactionEvent`s.\n    19→3. **Query Side** – pre-computed projections kept in `read_model.transactions`\n    20→   for low-latency reads / reporting.\n    21→4. **Saga Coordination** – publishes domain events to the message broker\n    22→   (`NATS JetStream`) and listens for compensation commands in case of failure\n    23→   (e.g. failed FX settlement, AML hit).\n    24→5. **Compliance Trail** – ships signed hash of every event to the `audit-vault`\n    25→   micro-service for tamper-proof archiving.\n    26→\n    27→---\n    28→\n    29→## 2. Service APIs\n    30→\n    31→| Method                            | Transport | Auth        | Description                              |\n    32→|----------------------------------|-----------|-------------|------------------------------------------|\n    33→| POST /v1/transactions            | REST      | JWT + mTLS  | Initiate a new transaction command       |\n    34→| GET  /v1/transactions/{id}       | REST      | JWT + mTLS  | Fetch aggregate / projections            |\n    35→| gRPC `TransactionService.Create` | gRPC      | mutual TLS  | Low-latency ingestion for internal calls |\n    36→\n    37→> NOTE  \n    38→> Public SDKs wrap these endpoints and transparently handle retries,\n    39→> idempotency keys, and saga correlation IDs.\n    40→\n    41→---\n    42→\n    43→## 3. Quick-Start (local)\n    44→\n    45→```bash\n    46→# 1. Clone mono-repo\n    47→git clone git@github.com:crowdpay/connect.git\n    48→cd connect/services/transaction_service\n    49→\n    50→# 2. Responsibilities\n\n1. **Command Side** – accepts _Transfer_, _Top-Up_, _Withdrawal_ or _FX Swap_\n   commands, validates them against:\n   • risk‐engine, • KYC state, • account limits, • FX quotation TTL.\n2. **Event Store** – append-only stream (`postgresql` → `wal2json` logical\n   slot) storing immutable `TransactionEvent`s.\n3. **Query Side** – pre-computed projections kept in `read_model.transactions`\n   for low-latency reads / reporting.\n4. **Saga Coordination** – publishes domain events to the message broker\n   (`NATS JetStream`) and listens for compensation commands in case of failure\n   (e.g. failed FX settlement, AML hit).\n5. **Compliance Trail** – ships signed hash of every event to the `audit-vault`\n   micro-service for tamper-proof archiving.\n\n---\n\n## 2. Service APIs\n\n| Method                            | Transport | Auth        | Description                              |\n|----------------------------------|-----------|-------------|------------------------------------------|\n| POST /v1/transactions            | REST      | JWT + mTLS  | Initiate a new transaction command       |\n| GET  /v1/transactions/{id}       | REST      | JWT + mTLS  | Fetch aggregate / projections            |\n| gRPC `TransactionService.Create` | gRPC      | mutual TLS  | Low-latency ingestion for internal calls |\n\n> NOTE  \n> Public SDKs wrap these endpoints and transparently handle retries,\n> idempotency keys, and saga correlation IDs.\n\n---\n\n## 3.
