{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-5-20251101","id":"msg_01NynsR7fkBrqBLVPWCBGkhN","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01LV1WBMAsR4j2B99HqE9h1J","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/logs/agent/solution.md","content":"# INSTANT_SETTLEMENT_PROPOSAL.md\n\n## Architectural Proposal: \"Instant Settlements\" Feature for CrowdPay Connect\n\n**Author:** Lead Architect\n**Version:** 1.0\n**Date:** 2026-02-02\n**Status:** Draft - For Review\n\n---\n\n## Executive Summary\n\nThis document proposes an architectural approach to integrate the \"Instant Settlement\" premium feature into CrowdPay Connect, enabling select users to have their transactions settled in near real-time through integration with a new third-party provider, **InstaPay**. The proposal addresses how to incorporate this synchronous settlement path while preserving the platform's existing architectural principles: event-driven design, Saga-based transaction orchestration, and event-sourced ledger integrity.\n\n---\n\n## 1. Analysis of the Current Architecture\n\n### 1.1 Standard Payment Settlement Flow\n\nThe current CrowdPay Connect platform processes payments through a **distributed Saga pattern** with multiple asynchronous steps designed for resilience and standard settlement timelines (T+2 days). The flow is orchestrated by the `transaction_service` and involves several bounded contexts:\n\n**Key Services Involved:**\n1. **Transaction Service** - Orchestrates the payment Saga, manages saga state, publishes domain events\n2. **Wallet Service** - Manages wallet balances via an immutable, append-only double-entry ledger\n3. **Risk & Compliance Service** - Performs real-time KYC, AML, and fraud checks\n4. **KYC Service** - Verifies customer identity\n5. **FX Service** - Handles currency conversion for multi-currency payments\n\n**Current Payment Saga Steps** (as defined in `services/transaction_service/app/sagas/payment_saga.py:238-273`):\n```\n1. KYC Verification      → Verify payer has valid KYC profile\n2. Risk Assessment       → Real-time transaction risk evaluation\n3. Debit Payer          → Reserve/debit funds from payer's wallet\n4. Currency Conversion   → Convert if source/target currencies differ\n5. Credit CrowdPod      → Credit funds to destination wallet\n6. Settlement           → Mark transaction as settled (T+2 timeline)\n```\n\nEach step has an associated **compensation action** that executes in reverse order if any step fails, ensuring eventual consistency (see `payment_saga.py:275-306`).\n\n### 1.2 Saga Pattern Implementation\n\nThe platform uses an **orchestration-based Saga pattern** (as documented in `docs/architecture/saga_pattern.md`) rather than choreography for monetary flows. This provides:\n\n- **Strong ordering guarantees** - Steps execute sequentially in defined order\n- **Centralized timeout/retry/compensation logic** - Single point of control\n- **Auditability** - All state transitions are tracked in `saga_states` table\n\nThe `SagaCoordinator` (`services/transaction_service/app/events/saga_coordinator.py:222-333`) manages:\n- Saga lifecycle (PENDING → IN_PROGRESS → COMPLETED/FAILED/COMPENSATED)\n- Step execution with async/await patterns\n- Automatic compensation on failure\n\n### 1.3 Event Sourcing & CQRS\n\nThe platform follows **CQRS with Event Sourcing** (see `docs/architecture/cqrs_event_sourcing.md`):\n\n- **Command Side**: Validates commands, applies domain logic, appends immutable events to PostgreSQL Event Store\n- **Event Bus**: Kafka topic `crowdpay.events` distributes events via logical replication\n- **Read Side**: Projectors consume events to update materialized views in ElasticSearch, Redis, ClickHouse\n\nThe `wallet_service/app/core/ledger.py` implements an **immutable, double-entry ledger** where:\n- Every transfer creates two `LedgerEntry` records (DEBIT + CREDIT)\n- Entries go through states: PENDING → COMMITTED → (optionally) REVERSED\n- Changes are published as domain events (`ledger.entry.committed`)\n\n### 1.4 Risk Assessment Architecture\n\nThe `risk_compliance_service` (`app/core/rule_engine.py` and `app/api/v1/assessment.py`) provides:\n\n- **Rule Engine**: Evaluates configurable compliance rules (KYC status, amount thresholds, cross-border checks)\n- **Decisions**: APPROVE, MANUAL_REVIEW, REJECT\n- **Risk Levels**: LOW, MEDIUM, HIGH, CRITICAL\n- **Async Assessment API**: Accepts requests, runs evaluation in background, publishes results via events\n\n**Key Files Analyzed:**\n- `docs/architecture/overview.md` - Macro architecture and service responsibilities\n- `docs/architecture/saga_pattern.md` - Detailed Saga implementation with code examples\n- `docs/architecture/cqrs_event_sourcing.md` - Event sourcing and CQRS patterns\n- `services/transaction_service/app/sagas/payment_saga.py` - Payment Saga implementation\n- `services/transaction_service/app/events/saga_coordinator.py` - Saga orchestration engine\n- `services/transaction_service/app/models/saga_state.py` - Saga persistence model\n- `services/transaction_service/app/repositories/saga_repository.py` - Saga database operations\n- `services/wallet_service/app/core/ledger.py` - Ledger service and double-entry logic\n- `services/wallet_service/app/events/consumer.py` - Event consumption patterns\n- `services/risk_compliance_service/app/core/rule_engine.py` - Rule engine implementation\n- `services/risk_compliance_service/app/api/v1/assessment.py` - Risk assessment API\n\n---\n\n## 2. Proposed Solution: Instant Settlement Architecture\n\n### 2.1 Why Not Modify the Existing Saga?\n\n**Critical Anti-Pattern to Avoid**: Injecting a synchronous, blocking API call to InstaPay directly inside the existing `PaymentSaga` would be an architectural anti-pattern because:\n\n1. **Violates Saga Principles** - Sagas are designed for long-running, asynchronous distributed transactions with compensating actions. A blocking external call would compromise resilience.\n\n2. **Timeout Complexity** - The existing Saga has a 5-minute TTL (`saga_coordinator.py:156`). A synchronous InstaPay call with its own timeout would create nested timeout management.\n\n3. **Compensation Mismatch** - InstaPay's synchronous settlement cannot be cleanly compensated through the async compensation chain.\n\n4. **Blocking Event Loop** - Synchronous calls would block the async event loop used by the Saga coordinator.\n\n### 2.2 Proposed Pattern: Settlement Strategy\n\nI propose implementing a **Strategy Pattern** to segregate the synchronous (instant) and asynchronous (standard) settlement flows at the point of transaction initiation.\n\n```\n                            ┌─────────────────────────┐\n                            │  Transaction Request    │\n                            │  (with settlement_type) │\n                            └───────────┬─────────────┘\n                                        │\n                            ┌───────────▼─────────────┐\n                            │  SettlementStrategy     │\n                            │  Factory/Router         │\n                            └───────────┬─────────────┘\n                                        │\n              ┌─────────────────────────┼─────────────────────────┐\n              │                         │                         │\n    ┌─────────▼─────────┐     ┌────────▼────────┐     ┌──────────▼────────┐\n    │  StandardSettlement│     │  InstantSettlement│     │  Future Strategies│\n    │  Strategy          │     │  Strategy          │     │  (e.g., Scheduled) │\n    │  (Existing Saga)   │     │  (New Sync Flow)   │     │                    │\n    └────────────────────┘     └───────────────────┘     └────────────────────┘\n```\n\n### 2.3 InstantSettlementStrategy Design\n\nThe `InstantSettlementStrategy` will orchestrate a **synchronous, blocking sequence** of calls that is self-contained and separate from the async Saga:\n\n```python\n# services/transaction_service/app/strategies/instant_settlement.py\n\nclass InstantSettlementStrategy:\n    \"\"\"\n    Synchronous settlement strategy for premium instant settlements.\n    Executes a deterministic sequence with compensating transactions on failure.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(\n        self,\n        risk_service: RiskService,\n        wallet_service: WalletService,\n        instapay_client: InstaPayClient,\n        event_publisher: EventPublisher,\n    ):\n        self.risk = risk_service\n        self.wallet = wallet_service\n        self.instapay = instapay_client\n        self.publisher = event_publisher\n\n    async def execute(self, command: InstantPaymentCommand) -> SettlementResult:\n        \"\"\"\n        Execute synchronous instant settlement flow.\n\n        Flow:\n        1. Pre-authorization risk check (synchronous)\n        2. Reserve funds in payer's wallet\n        3. Call InstaPay API (synchronous external call)\n        4. On success: Publish InstantSettlementSucceeded event\n        5. On failure: Execute compensation (release reserved funds)\n        \"\"\"\n        reservation_id = None\n\n        try:\n            # Step 1: Synchronous pre-authorization risk check\n            risk_result = await self._pre_auth_risk_check(command)\n            if risk_result.decision == Decision.REJECT:\n                raise InstantSettlementRejected(\"Risk check failed\")\n\n            # Step 2: Reserve funds (with PENDING status)\n            reservation_id = await self._reserve_funds(command)\n\n            # Step 3: Synchronous InstaPay API call\n            instapay_response = await self._call_instapay(command)\n\n            # Step 4: Publish success event (triggers wallet ledger update)\n            await self._publish_success_event(command, instapay_response, reservation_id)\n\n            return SettlementResult(\n                status=SettlementStatus.COMPLETED,\n                transaction_id=command.transaction_id,\n                instapay_reference=instapay_response.reference,\n            )\n\n        except InstaPayError as e:\n            # Compensation: Release reserved funds\n            if reservation_id:\n                await self._release_reserved_funds(reservation_id, command)\n            raise InstantSettlementFailed(f\"InstaPay call failed: {e}\")\n```\n\n### 2.4 Transaction Service Modifications\n\nThe `transaction_service` needs a router/factory to determine which strategy to use:\n\n```python\n# services/transaction_service/app/services/settlement_router.py\n\nclass SettlementRouter:\n    \"\"\"\n    Routes payment requests to appropriate settlement strategy.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(\n        self,\n        standard_strategy: StandardSettlementStrategy,  # Wraps existing PaymentSaga\n        instant_strategy: InstantSettlementStrategy,\n    ):\n        self.strategies = {\n            SettlementType.STANDARD: standard_strategy,\n            SettlementType.INSTANT: instant_strategy,\n        }\n\n    async def route(self, command: PaymentCommand) -> SettlementResult:\n        \"\"\"\n        Determine and execute appropriate settlement strategy.\n        \"\"\"\n        settlement_type = self._determine_settlement_type(command)\n        strategy = self.strategies[settlement_type]\n        return await strategy.execute(command)\n\n    def _determine_settlement_type(self, command: PaymentCommand) -> SettlementType:\n        \"\"\"\n        Business rules for settlement type selection:\n        - User must be premium tier\n        - Transaction must be eligible (amount limits, currency support)\n        - Feature flag enabled\n        \"\"\"\n        if (\n            command.user_tier == UserTier.PREMIUM and\n            command.request_instant_settlement and\n            self._is_instant_eligible(command)\n        ):\n            return SettlementType.INSTANT\n        return SettlementType.STANDARD\n```\n\n### 2.5 InstaPay API Integration\n\nThe InstaPay integration should be wrapped in a dedicated client with:\n\n```python\n# services/transaction_service/app/integrations/instapay_client.py\n\nclass InstaPayClient:\n    \"\"\"\n    HTTP client for InstaPay external API.\n    Implements circuit breaker, retries, and timeout handling.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(\n        self,\n        base_url: str,\n        api_key: str,\n        timeout: float = 10.0,  # InstaPay SLA\n    ):\n        self.base_url = base_url\n        self.timeout = timeout\n        self._circuit_breaker = CircuitBreaker(\n            failure_threshold=5,\n            recovery_timeout=60,\n        )\n\n    @circuit_breaker_decorator\n    async def settle_instantly(\n        self,\n        transaction_id: str,\n        amount: Money,\n        payer_account: str,\n        payee_account: str,\n    ) -> InstaPayResponse:\n        \"\"\"\n        Synchronous call to InstaPay settlement API.\n        \"\"\"\n        async with httpx.AsyncClient(timeout=self.timeout) as client:\n            response = await client.post(\n                f\"{self.base_url}/v1/instant-settle\",\n                json={\n                    \"transaction_id\": transaction_id,\n                    \"amount\": str(amount.value),\n                    \"currency\": amount.currency,\n                    \"payer_account\": payer_account,\n                    \"payee_account\": payee_account,\n                },\n                headers={\"Authorization\": f\"Bearer {self.api_key}\"},\n            )\n            response.raise_for_status()\n            return InstaPayResponse.parse_obj(response.json())\n```\n\n### 2.6 Wallet Service Ledger Updates\n\n**Critical Design Decision**: To preserve the event-sourced nature of the wallet ledger, the `InstantSettlementStrategy` should **NOT** directly call wallet APIs to finalize ledger entries. Instead:\n\n1. The `InstantSettlementStrategy` publishes an **`InstantSettlementSucceeded`** event\n2. The `wallet_service` has a new event consumer that handles this trusted event\n3. The consumer finalizes the ledger entries (PENDING → COMMITTED)\n\n```python\n# services/wallet_service/app/events/instant_settlement_consumer.py\n\nclass InstantSettlementEventHandler:\n    \"\"\"\n    Handles InstantSettlementSucceeded events to finalize ledger entries.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    async def handle_instant_settlement_succeeded(\n        self,\n        event: InstantSettlementSucceeded,\n    ) -> None:\n        \"\"\"\n        Finalize ledger entries after successful instant settlement.\n\n        This event is highly trusted (comes from authenticated internal service)\n        and triggers the final state transition.\n        \"\"\"\n        # Mark reserved funds as committed\n        await self.ledger_repo.mark_committed(\n            entry_ids=[event.data[\"debit_entry_id\"]],\n        )\n\n        # Credit the payee\n        credit_entry = LedgerEntry(\n            id=uuid.uuid4(),\n            wallet_id=event.data[\"payee_wallet_id\"],\n            entry_type=EntryType.CREDIT,\n            money=Money(\n                amount=Decimal(event.data[\"amount\"]),\n                currency=Currency(event.data[\"currency\"]),\n            ),\n            external_txn_id=event.data[\"transaction_id\"],\n            status=EntryStatus.COMMITTED,  # Directly committed\n        )\n        await self.ledger_repo.save_entries([credit_entry])\n\n        # Publish ledger event for downstream projections\n        await self.publisher.publish(\n            \"ledger.instant.settled\",\n            {\n                \"transaction_id\": event.data[\"transaction_id\"],\n                \"instapay_reference\": event.data[\"instapay_reference\"],\n                \"settled_at\": datetime.utcnow().isoformat(),\n            },\n        )\n```\n\n**New Event Types Required:**\n```python\n# Add to wallet_service/app/events/consumer.py _EVENT_REGISTRY\n\nclass InstantSettlementSucceeded(Event):\n    type: str = \"instant.settlement.succeeded\"\n    data: Dict[str, Any]  # { transaction_id, debit_entry_id, payee_wallet_id, amount, currency, instapay_reference }\n\nclass InstantSettlementFailed(Event):\n    type: str = \"instant.settlement.failed\"\n    data: Dict[str, Any]  # { transaction_id, reservation_id, reason }\n```\n\n### 2.7 Risk & Compliance Service Modifications\n\nThe `risk_compliance_service` needs enhancements for instant settlement scenarios:\n\n**2.7.1 New Rule Set for Instant Settlements**\n\nInstant settlements carry higher risk due to their irrevocable nature. New rules should be added:\n\n```yaml\n# config/rules/instant_settlement_rules.yaml\n- name: \"InstantSettlement_HighAmount\"\n  description: \"Flag instant settlements over $5k for manual review\"\n  priority: 100\n  conditions:\n    - \"transaction.settlement_type == 'INSTANT'\"\n    - \"transaction.amount > 5000\"\n  actions:\n    risk_level: \"HIGH\"\n    decision: \"MANUAL_REVIEW\"\n    alert_code: \"INST-001\"\n\n- name: \"InstantSettlement_NewUser\"\n  description: \"Reject instant settlements for users < 30 days old\"\n  priority: 110\n  conditions:\n    - \"transaction.settlement_type == 'INSTANT'\"\n    - \"user.account_age_days < 30\"\n  actions:\n    risk_level: \"CRITICAL\"\n    decision: \"REJECT\"\n    alert_code: \"INST-002\"\n\n- name: \"InstantSettlement_VelocityCheck\"\n  description: \"Flag rapid successive instant settlements\"\n  priority: 95\n  conditions:\n    - \"transaction.settlement_type == 'INSTANT'\"\n    - \"user.instant_settlements_24h > 3\"\n  actions:\n    risk_level: \"HIGH\"\n    decision: \"MANUAL_REVIEW\"\n    alert_code: \"INST-003\"\n```\n\n**2.7.2 Synchronous Risk Assessment Endpoint**\n\nFor instant settlements, we need a **synchronous** risk check (not the async background job pattern in current `assessment.py`):\n\n```python\n# services/risk_compliance_service/app/api/v1/instant_assessment.py\n\n@router.post(\n    \"/instant\",\n    response_model=InstantAssessmentOut,\n    status_code=status.HTTP_200_OK,  # Synchronous response\n    summary=\"Synchronous risk assessment for instant settlements\",\n)\nasync def instant_assessment(\n    payload: InstantAssessmentIn,\n    engine: _RiskEngine = Depends(get_risk_engine),\n) -> InstantAssessmentOut:\n    \"\"\"\n    Synchronous risk assessment designed for instant settlement flows.\n    Must respond within 500ms SLA.\n    \"\"\"\n    # Apply instant-settlement-specific rules\n    score = engine.score_instant(\n        amount=payload.amount,\n        currency_code=payload.currency_code,\n        user_reputation=_fetch_user_reputation(payload.user_id),\n        settlement_type=\"INSTANT\",\n    )\n\n    decision = engine.decide_instant(score)\n\n    return InstantAssessmentOut(\n        decision=decision,\n        score=score,\n        reference_id=payload.reference_id,\n    )\n```\n\n---\n\n## 3. Failure Handling & Compensation Strategy\n\n### 3.1 Failure Scenarios\n\nThe InstantSettlementStrategy must handle failures at each stage:\n\n| Failure Point | State | Compensation Action |\n|---------------|-------|---------------------|\n| Pre-auth risk check fails | No funds reserved | None - reject transaction |\n| Funds reservation fails | No funds reserved | None - insufficient funds error |\n| InstaPay API fails | Funds reserved (PENDING) | Release reserved funds |\n| InstaPay timeout | Funds reserved, unknown external state | Manual reconciliation queue |\n| Event publish fails | Settlement complete externally | Retry event publish with idempotency |\n\n### 3.2 Compensation Implementation\n\n```python\n# services/transaction_service/app/strategies/instant_settlement.py\n\nclass InstantSettlementStrategy:\n\n    async def _release_reserved_funds(\n        self,\n        reservation_id: str,\n        command: InstantPaymentCommand,\n    ) -> None:\n        \"\"\"\n        Compensation action: Release reserved funds back to payer.\n        Published as event for eventual consistency.\n        \"\"\"\n        await self.publisher.publish(\n            event_name=\"instant.settlement.compensation.release_funds\",\n            payload={\n                \"reservation_id\": reservation_id,\n                \"transaction_id\": command.transaction_id,\n                \"payer_wallet_id\": command.payer_wallet_id,\n                \"amount\": str(command.amount.value),\n                \"currency\": command.amount.currency,\n                \"reason\": \"InstaPay settlement failed\",\n            },\n        )\n\n        # For critical path, also make synchronous call to ensure immediate release\n        await self.wallet.release_reservation(reservation_id)\n\n    async def _handle_instapay_timeout(\n        self,\n        command: InstantPaymentCommand,\n        reservation_id: str,\n    ) -> None:\n        \"\"\"\n        Handle InstaPay timeout - uncertain external state.\n        Queue for manual reconciliation.\n        \"\"\"\n        await self.publisher.publish(\n            event_name=\"instant.settlement.reconciliation.required\",\n            payload={\n                \"transaction_id\": command.transaction_id,\n                \"reservation_id\": reservation_id,\n                \"status\": \"TIMEOUT\",\n                \"requires_manual_check\": True,\n            },\n        )\n\n        # Mark transaction as PENDING_RECONCILIATION\n        raise InstantSettlementTimeout(\n            \"InstaPay timeout - queued for reconciliation\"\n        )\n```\n\n### 3.3 Wallet Service Compensation Handler\n\n```python\n# services/wallet_service/app/events/instant_settlement_consumer.py\n\nasync def handle_instant_settlement_compensation(\n    self,\n    event: InstantSettlementCompensation,\n) -> None:\n    \"\"\"\n    Handle compensation event - release reserved funds.\n    \"\"\"\n    reservation_id = event.data[\"reservation_id\"]\n\n    # Mark the PENDING ledger entry as REVERSED\n    await self.ledger_repo.mark_reversed(\n        entry_ids=[reservation_id],\n        reason=event.data[\"reason\"],\n        failed=True,\n    )\n\n    # Publish compensation complete event\n    await self.publisher.publish(\n        \"ledger.reservation.released\",\n        {\n            \"reservation_id\": reservation_id,\n            \"transaction_id\": event.data[\"transaction_id\"],\n            \"released_at\": datetime.utcnow().isoformat(),\n        },\n    )\n```\n\n---\n\n## 4. Sequence Diagram: Successful Instant Settlement\n\n```mermaid\nsequenceDiagram\n    autonumber\n    actor User\n    participant API as API Gateway\n    participant TXN as Transaction Service<br/>(SettlementRouter)\n    participant INST as InstantSettlement<br/>Strategy\n    participant RISK as Risk & Compliance<br/>Service\n    participant WALLET as Wallet Service\n    participant INSTAPAY as InstaPay<br/>(External)\n    participant KAFKA as Event Bus<br/>(Kafka)\n\n    User->>API: POST /transactions<br/>{settlement_type: \"INSTANT\"}\n    API->>TXN: route(PaymentCommand)\n\n    Note over TXN: Determine strategy based on<br/>user tier & eligibility\n\n    TXN->>INST: execute(InstantPaymentCommand)\n\n    rect rgb(240, 248, 255)\n        Note over INST,RISK: Step 1: Synchronous Risk Check\n        INST->>RISK: POST /api/v1/assessment/instant\n        RISK-->>INST: {decision: APPROVED, score: 0.2}\n    end\n\n    rect rgb(240, 255, 240)\n        Note over INST,WALLET: Step 2: Reserve Funds\n        INST->>WALLET: POST /wallet/v1/reserve\n        WALLET-->>INST: {reservation_id: \"res_123\",<br/>status: PENDING}\n    end\n\n    rect rgb(255, 248, 240)\n        Note over INST,INSTAPAY: Step 3: Synchronous External Settlement\n        INST->>INSTAPAY: POST /v1/instant-settle\n        Note over INSTAPAY: Process settlement<br/>in real-time\n        INSTAPAY-->>INST: {reference: \"IP_456\",<br/>status: SETTLED}\n    end\n\n    rect rgb(248, 240, 255)\n        Note over INST,KAFKA: Step 4: Publish Success Event\n        INST->>KAFKA: publish(InstantSettlementSucceeded)\n        KAFKA-->>INST: ack\n    end\n\n    INST-->>TXN: SettlementResult{COMPLETED}\n    TXN-->>API: 200 OK\n    API-->>User: Transaction Settled<br/>{instapay_ref: \"IP_456\"}\n\n    Note over KAFKA,WALLET: Async: Event-Driven Ledger Update\n\n    KAFKA->>WALLET: consume(InstantSettlementSucceeded)\n\n    rect rgb(240, 255, 240)\n        Note over WALLET: Finalize Ledger Entries\n        WALLET->>WALLET: mark_committed(debit_entry)\n        WALLET->>WALLET: create_credit_entry(COMMITTED)\n    end\n\n    WALLET->>KAFKA: publish(ledger.instant.settled)\n```\n\n---\n\n## 5. Implementation Roadmap\n\n### Phase 1: Foundation (Sprint 1-2)\n- [ ] Add `settlement_type` field to `PaymentCommand` model\n- [ ] Implement `SettlementRouter` with strategy factory\n- [ ] Create `StandardSettlementStrategy` wrapper around existing `PaymentSaga`\n- [ ] Add database migrations for instant settlement metadata\n\n### Phase 2: InstaPay Integration (Sprint 3-4)\n- [ ] Implement `InstaPayClient` with circuit breaker\n- [ ] Create `InstantSettlementStrategy` class\n- [ ] Add InstaPay configuration and credentials management\n- [ ] Implement synchronous risk assessment endpoint\n\n### Phase 3: Wallet Service Updates (Sprint 4-5)\n- [ ] Add `InstantSettlementSucceeded` event handler\n- [ ] Implement compensation handlers for fund release\n- [ ] Add new event types to consumer registry\n- [ ] Update ledger service for instant settlement entries\n\n### Phase 4: Risk & Compliance (Sprint 5-6)\n- [ ] Create instant settlement rule set\n- [ ] Implement velocity checking for instant settlements\n- [ ] Add synchronous assessment endpoint\n- [ ] Configure stricter thresholds for instant payments\n\n### Phase 5: Testing & Rollout (Sprint 6-7)\n- [ ] Integration tests with InstaPay sandbox\n- [ ] Chaos testing for failure scenarios\n- [ ] Performance testing for latency SLAs\n- [ ] Gradual rollout with feature flags\n\n---\n\n## 6. Summary of Key Architectural Decisions\n\n| Decision | Rationale |\n|----------|-----------|\n| **Separate Strategy** instead of modifying Saga | Preserves async resilience of existing flow; avoids anti-pattern of blocking calls in Saga |\n| **Synchronous Risk Check** for instant path | Instant settlements require immediate decision; cannot wait for async background processing |\n| **Event-driven Ledger Update** | Preserves event-sourcing integrity; wallet service remains single source of truth for ledger |\n| **Compensation via Events** | Ensures auditability; enables retry and idempotency; aligns with existing patterns |\n| **Circuit Breaker** for InstaPay | Protects system from external service degradation; enables graceful fallback |\n| **Stricter Risk Rules** for instant | Irrevocable nature of instant settlements requires higher scrutiny |\n\n---\n\n## 7. The proposal addresses how to incorporate this synchronous settlement path while preserving the platform's existing architectural principles: event-driven design, Saga-based transaction orchestration, and event-sourced ledger integrity.\n\n---\n\n## 1. Analysis of the Current Architecture\n\n### 1.1 Standard Payment Settlement Flow\n\nThe current CrowdPay Connect platform processes payments through a **distributed Saga pattern** with multiple asynchronous steps designed for resilience and standard settlement timelines (T+2 days). The flow is orchestrated by the `transaction_service` and involves several bounded contexts:\n\n**Key Services Involved:**\n1. **Transaction Service** - Orchestrates the payment Saga, manages saga state, publishes domain events\n2. **Wallet Service** - Manages wallet balances via an immutable, append-only double-entry ledger\n3. **Risk & Compliance Service** - Performs real-time KYC, AML, and fraud checks\n4. **KYC Service** - Verifies customer identity\n5. **FX Service** - Handles currency conversion for multi-currency payments\n\n**Current Payment Saga Steps** (as defined in `services/transaction_service/app/sagas/payment_saga.py:238-273`):\n```\n1. KYC Verification      → Verify payer has valid KYC profile\n2. Risk Assessment       → Real-time transaction risk evaluation\n3. Debit Payer          → Reserve/debit funds from payer's wallet\n4. Currency Conversion   → Convert if source/target currencies differ\n5. Credit CrowdPod      → Credit funds to destination wallet\n6. Settlement           → Mark transaction as settled (T+2 timeline)\n```\n\nEach step has an associated **compensation action** that executes in reverse order if any step fails, ensuring eventual consistency (see `payment_saga.py:275-306`).\n\n### 1.2 Saga Pattern Implementation\n\nThe platform uses an **orchestration-based Saga pattern** (as documented in `docs/architecture/saga_pattern.md`) rather than choreography for monetary flows. This provides:\n\n- **Strong ordering guarantees** - Steps execute sequentially in defined order\n- **Centralized timeout/retry/compensation logic** - Single point of control\n- **Auditability** - All state transitions are tracked in `saga_states` table\n\nThe `SagaCoordinator` (`services/transaction_service/app/events/saga_coordinator.py:222-333`) manages:\n- Saga lifecycle (PENDING → IN_PROGRESS → COMPLETED/FAILED/COMPENSATED)\n- Step execution with async/await patterns\n- Automatic compensation on failure\n\n### 1.3 Event Sourcing & CQRS\n\nThe platform follows **CQRS with Event Sourcing** (see `docs/architecture/cqrs_event_sourcing.md`):\n\n- **Command Side**: Validates commands, applies domain logic, appends immutable events to PostgreSQL Event Store\n- **Event Bus**: Kafka topic `crowdpay.events` distributes events via logical replication\n- **Read Side**: Projectors consume events to update materialized views in ElasticSearch, Redis, ClickHouse\n\nThe `wallet_service/app/core/ledger.py` implements an **immutable, double-entry ledger** where:\n- Every transfer creates two `LedgerEntry` records (DEBIT + CREDIT)\n- Entries go through states: PENDING → COMMITTED → (optionally) REVERSED\n- Changes are published as domain events (`ledger.entry.committed`)\n\n### 1.4 Risk Assessment Architecture\n\nThe `risk_compliance_service` (`app/core/rule_engine.py` and `app/api/v1/assessment.py`) provides:\n\n- **Rule Engine**: Evaluates configurable compliance rules (KYC status, amount thresholds, cross-border checks)\n- **Decisions**: APPROVE, MANUAL_REVIEW, REJECT\n- **Risk Levels**: LOW, MEDIUM, HIGH, CRITICAL\n- **Async Assessment API**: Accepts requests, runs evaluation in background, publishes results via events\n\n**Key Files Analyzed:**\n- `docs/architecture/overview.md` - Macro architecture and service responsibilities\n- `docs/architecture/saga_pattern.md` - Detailed Saga implementation with code examples\n- `docs/architecture/cqrs_event_sourcing.md` - Event sourcing and CQRS patterns\n- `services/transaction_service/app/sagas/payment_saga.py` - Payment Saga implementation\n- `services/transaction_service/app/events/saga_coordinator.py` - Saga orchestration engine\n- `services/transaction_service/app/models/saga_state.py` - Saga persistence model\n- `services/transaction_service/app/repositories/saga_repository.py` - Saga database operations\n- `services/wallet_service/app/core/ledger.py` - Ledger service and double-entry logic\n- `services/wallet_service/app/events/consumer.py` - Event consumption patterns\n- `services/risk_compliance_service/app/core/rule_engine.py` - Rule engine implementation\n- `services/risk_compliance_service/app/api/v1/assessment.py` - Risk assessment API\n\n---\n\n## 2. Proposed Solution: Instant Settlement Architecture\n\n### 2.1 Why Not Modify the Existing Saga?\n\n**Critical Anti-Pattern to Avoid**: Injecting a synchronous, blocking API call to InstaPay directly inside the existing `PaymentSaga` would be an architectural anti-pattern because:\n\n1. **Violates Saga Principles** - Sagas are designed for long-running, asynchronous distributed transactions with compensating actions. A blocking external call would compromise resilience.\n\n2. **Timeout Complexity** - The existing Saga has a 5-minute TTL (`saga_coordinator.py:156`). A synchronous InstaPay call with its own timeout would create nested timeout management.\n\n3. **Compensation Mismatch** - InstaPay's synchronous settlement cannot be cleanly compensated through the async compensation chain.\n\n4. **Blocking Event Loop** - Synchronous calls would block the async event loop used by the Saga coordinator.\n\n### 2.2 Proposed Pattern: Settlement Strategy\n\nI propose implementing a **Strategy Pattern** to segregate the synchronous (instant) and asynchronous (standard) settlement flows at the point of transaction initiation.\n\n```\n                            ┌─────────────────────────┐\n                            │  Transaction Request    │\n                            │  (with settlement_type) │\n                            └───────────┬─────────────┘\n                                        │\n                            ┌───────────▼─────────────┐\n                            │  SettlementStrategy     │\n                            │  Factory/Router         │\n                            └───────────┬─────────────┘\n                                        │\n              ┌─────────────────────────┼─────────────────────────┐\n              │                         │                         │\n    ┌─────────▼─────────┐     ┌────────▼────────┐     ┌──────────▼────────┐\n    │  StandardSettlement│     │  InstantSettlement│     │  Future Strategies│\n    │  Strategy          │     │  Strategy          │     │  (e.g., Scheduled) │\n    │  (Existing Saga)   │     │  (New Sync Flow)   │     │                    │\n    └────────────────────┘     └───────────────────┘     └────────────────────┘\n```\n\n### 2.3 InstantSettlementStrategy Design\n\nThe `InstantSettlementStrategy` will orchestrate a **synchronous, blocking sequence** of calls that is self-contained and separate from the async Saga:\n\n```python\n# services/transaction_service/app/strategies/instant_settlement.py\n\nclass InstantSettlementStrategy:\n    \"\"\"\n    Synchronous settlement strategy for premium instant settlements.\n    Executes a deterministic sequence with compensating transactions on failure.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(\n        self,\n        risk_service: RiskService,\n        wallet_service: WalletService,\n        instapay_client: InstaPayClient,\n        event_publisher: EventPublisher,\n    ):\n        self.risk = risk_service\n        self.wallet = wallet_service\n        self.instapay = instapay_client\n        self.publisher = event_publisher\n\n    async def execute(self, command: InstantPaymentCommand) -> SettlementResult:\n        \"\"\"\n        Execute synchronous instant settlement flow.\n\n        Flow:\n        1. Pre-authorization risk check (synchronous)\n        2. Reserve funds in payer's wallet\n        3. Call InstaPay API (synchronous external call)\n        4. On success: Publish InstantSettlementSucceeded event\n        5. On failure: Execute compensation (release reserved funds)\n        \"\"\"\n        reservation_id = None\n\n        try:\n            # Step 1: Synchronous pre-authorization risk check\n            risk_result = await self._pre_auth_risk_check(command)\n            if risk_result.decision == Decision.REJECT:\n                raise InstantSettlementRejected(\"Risk check failed\")\n\n            # Step 2: Reserve funds (with PENDING status)\n            reservation_id = await self._reserve_funds(command)\n\n            # Step 3: Synchronous InstaPay API call\n            instapay_response = await self._call_instapay(command)\n\n            # Step 4: Publish success event (triggers wallet ledger update)\n            await self._publish_success_event(command, instapay_response, reservation_id)\n\n            return SettlementResult(\n                status=SettlementStatus.COMPLETED,\n                transaction_id=command.transaction_id,\n                instapay_reference=instapay_response.reference,\n            )\n\n        except InstaPayError as e:\n            # Compensation: Release reserved funds\n            if reservation_id:\n                await self._release_reserved_funds(reservation_id, command)\n            raise InstantSettlementFailed(f\"InstaPay call failed: {e}\")\n```\n\n### 2.4 Transaction Service Modifications\n\nThe `transaction_service` needs a router/factory to determine which strategy to use:\n\n```python\n# services/transaction_service/app/services/settlement_router.py\n\nclass SettlementRouter:\n    \"\"\"\n    Routes payment requests to appropriate settlement strategy.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(\n        self,\n        standard_strategy: StandardSettlementStrategy,  # Wraps existing PaymentSaga\n        instant_strategy: InstantSettlementStrategy,\n    ):\n        self.strategies = {\n            SettlementType.STANDARD: standard_strategy,\n            SettlementType.INSTANT: instant_strategy,\n        }\n\n    async def route(self, command: PaymentCommand) -> SettlementResult:\n        \"\"\"\n        Determine and execute appropriate settlement strategy.\n        \"\"\"\n        settlement_type = self._determine_settlement_type(command)\n        strategy = self.strategies[settlement_type]\n        return await strategy.execute(command)\n\n    def _determine_settlement_type(self, command: PaymentCommand) -> SettlementType:\n        \"\"\"\n        Business rules for settlement type selection:\n        - User must be premium tier\n        - Transaction must be eligible (amount limits, currency support)\n        - Feature flag enabled\n        \"\"\"\n        if (\n            command.user_tier == UserTier.PREMIUM and\n            command.request_instant_settlement and\n            self._is_instant_eligible(command)\n        ):\n            return SettlementType.INSTANT\n        return SettlementType.STANDARD\n```\n\n### 2.5 InstaPay API Integration\n\nThe InstaPay integration should be wrapped in a dedicated client with:\n\n```python\n# services/transaction_service/app/integrations/instapay_client.py\n\nclass InstaPayClient:\n    \"\"\"\n    HTTP client for InstaPay external API.\n    Implements circuit breaker, retries, and timeout handling.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(\n        self,\n        base_url: str,\n        api_key: str,\n        timeout: float = 10.0,  # InstaPay SLA\n    ):\n        self.base_url = base_url\n        self.timeout = timeout\n        self._circuit_breaker = CircuitBreaker(\n            failure_threshold=5,\n            recovery_timeout=60,\n        )\n\n    @circuit_breaker_decorator\n    async def settle_instantly(\n        self,\n        transaction_id: str,\n        amount: Money,\n        payer_account: str,\n        payee_account: str,\n    ) -> InstaPayResponse:\n        \"\"\"\n        Synchronous call to InstaPay settlement API.\n        \"\"\"\n        async with httpx.AsyncClient(timeout=self.timeout) as client:\n            response = await client.post(\n                f\"{self.base_url}/v1/instant-settle\",\n                json={\n                    \"transaction_id\": transaction_id,\n                    \"amount\": str(amount.value),\n                    \"currency\": amount.currency,\n                    \"payer_account\": payer_account,\n                    \"payee_account\": payee_account,\n                },\n                headers={\"Authorization\": f\"Bearer {self.api_key}\"},\n            )\n            response.raise_for_status()\n            return InstaPayResponse.parse_obj(response.json())\n```\n\n### 2.6 Wallet Service Ledger Updates\n\n**Critical Design Decision**: To preserve the event-sourced nature of the wallet ledger, the `InstantSettlementStrategy` should **NOT** directly call wallet APIs to finalize ledger entries. Instead:\n\n1. The `InstantSettlementStrategy` publishes an **`InstantSettlementSucceeded`** event\n2. The `wallet_service` has a new event consumer that handles this trusted event\n3. The consumer finalizes the ledger entries (PENDING → COMMITTED)\n\n```python\n# services/wallet_service/app/events/instant_settlement_consumer.py\n\nclass InstantSettlementEventHandler:\n    \"\"\"\n    Handles InstantSettlementSucceeded events to finalize ledger entries.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    async def handle_instant_settlement_succeeded(\n        self,\n        event: InstantSettlementSucceeded,\n    ) -> None:\n        \"\"\"\n        Finalize ledger entries after successful instant settlement.\n\n        This event is highly trusted (comes from authenticated internal service)\n        and triggers the final state transition.\n        \"\"\"\n        # Mark reserved funds as committed\n        await self.ledger_repo.mark_committed(\n            entry_ids=[event.data[\"debit_entry_id\"]],\n        )\n\n        # Credit the payee\n        credit_entry = LedgerEntry(\n            id=uuid.uuid4(),\n            wallet_id=event.data[\"payee_wallet_id\"],\n            entry_type=EntryType.CREDIT,\n            money=Money(\n                amount=Decimal(event.data[\"amount\"]),\n                currency=Currency(event.data[\"currency\"]),\n            ),\n            external_txn_id=event.data[\"transaction_id\"],\n            status=EntryStatus.COMMITTED,  # Directly committed\n        )\n        await self.ledger_repo.save_entries([credit_entry])\n\n        # Publish ledger event for downstream projections\n        await self.publisher.publish(\n            \"ledger.instant.settled\",\n            {\n                \"transaction_id\": event.data[\"transaction_id\"],\n                \"instapay_reference\": event.data[\"instapay_reference\"],\n                \"settled_at\": datetime.utcnow().isoformat(),\n            },\n        )\n```\n\n**New Event Types Required:**\n```python\n# Add to wallet_service/app/events/consumer.py _EVENT_REGISTRY\n\nclass InstantSettlementSucceeded(Event):\n    type: str = \"instant.settlement.succeeded\"\n    data: Dict[str, Any]  # { transaction_id, debit_entry_id, payee_wallet_id, amount, currency, instapay_reference }\n\nclass InstantSettlementFailed(Event):\n    type: str = \"instant.settlement.failed\"\n    data: Dict[str, Any]  # { transaction_id, reservation_id, reason }\n```\n\n### 2.7 Risk & Compliance Service Modifications\n\nThe `risk_compliance_service` needs enhancements for instant settlement scenarios:\n\n**2.7.1 New Rule Set for Instant Settlements**\n\nInstant settlements carry higher risk due to their irrevocable nature. New rules should be added:\n\n```yaml\n# config/rules/instant_settlement_rules.yaml\n- name: \"InstantSettlement_HighAmount\"\n  description: \"Flag instant settlements over $5k for manual review\"\n  priority: 100\n  conditions:\n    - \"transaction.settlement_type == 'INSTANT'\"\n    - \"transaction.amount > 5000\"\n  actions:\n    risk_level: \"HIGH\"\n    decision: \"MANUAL_REVIEW\"\n    alert_code: \"INST-001\"\n\n- name: \"InstantSettlement_NewUser\"\n  description: \"Reject instant settlements for users < 30 days old\"\n  priority: 110\n  conditions:\n    - \"transaction.settlement_type == 'INSTANT'\"\n    - \"user.account_age_days < 30\"\n  actions:\n    risk_level: \"CRITICAL\"\n    decision: \"REJECT\"\n    alert_code: \"INST-002\"\n\n- name: \"InstantSettlement_VelocityCheck\"\n  description: \"Flag rapid successive instant settlements\"\n  priority: 95\n  conditions:\n    - \"transaction.settlement_type == 'INSTANT'\"\n    - \"user.instant_settlements_24h > 3\"\n  actions:\n    risk_level: \"HIGH\"\n    decision: \"MANUAL_REVIEW\"\n    alert_code: \"INST-003\"\n```\n\n**2.7.2 Synchronous Risk Assessment Endpoint**\n\nFor instant settlements, we need a **synchronous** risk check (not the async background job pattern in current `assessment.py`):\n\n```python\n# services/risk_compliance_service/app/api/v1/instant_assessment.py\n\n@router.post(\n    \"/instant\",\n    response_model=InstantAssessmentOut,\n    status_code=status.HTTP_200_OK,  # Synchronous response\n    summary=\"Synchronous risk assessment for instant settlements\",\n)\nasync def instant_assessment(\n    payload: InstantAssessmentIn,\n    engine: _RiskEngine = Depends(get_risk_engine),\n) -> InstantAssessmentOut:\n    \"\"\"\n    Synchronous risk assessment designed for instant settlement flows.\n    Must respond within 500ms SLA.\n    \"\"\"\n    # Apply instant-settlement-specific rules\n    score = engine.score_instant(\n        amount=payload.amount,\n        currency_code=payload.currency_code,\n        user_reputation=_fetch_user_reputation(payload.user_id),\n        settlement_type=\"INSTANT\",\n    )\n\n    decision = engine.decide_instant(score)\n\n    return InstantAssessmentOut(\n        decision=decision,\n        score=score,\n        reference_id=payload.reference_id,\n    )\n```\n\n---\n\n## 3. Failure Handling & Compensation Strategy\n\n### 3.1 Failure Scenarios\n\nThe InstantSettlementStrategy must handle failures at each stage:\n\n| Failure Point | State | Compensation Action |\n|---------------|-------|---------------------|\n| Pre-auth risk check fails | No funds reserved | None - reject transaction |\n| Funds reservation fails | No funds reserved | None - insufficient funds error |\n| InstaPay API fails | Funds reserved (PENDING) | Release reserved funds |\n| InstaPay timeout | Funds reserved, unknown external state | Manual reconciliation queue |\n| Event publish fails | Settlement complete externally | Retry event publish with idempotency |\n\n### 3.2 Compensation Implementation\n\n```python\n# services/transaction_service/app/strategies/instant_settlement.py\n\nclass InstantSettlementStrategy:\n\n    async def _release_reserved_funds(\n        self,\n        reservation_id: str,\n        command: InstantPaymentCommand,\n    ) -> None:\n        \"\"\"\n        Compensation action: Release reserved funds back to payer.\n        Published as event for eventual consistency.\n        \"\"\"\n        await self.publisher.publish(\n            event_name=\"instant.settlement.compensation.release_funds\",\n            payload={\n                \"reservation_id\": reservation_id,\n                \"transaction_id\": command.transaction_id,\n                \"payer_wallet_id\": command.payer_wallet_id,\n                \"amount\": str(command.amount.value),\n                \"currency\": command.amount.currency,\n                \"reason\": \"InstaPay settlement failed\",\n            },\n        )\n\n        # For critical path, also make synchronous call to ensure immediate release\n        await self.wallet.release_reservation(reservation_id)\n\n    async def _handle_instapay_timeout(\n        self,\n        command: InstantPaymentCommand,\n        reservation_id: str,\n    ) -> None:\n        \"\"\"\n        Handle InstaPay timeout - uncertain external state.\n        Queue for manual reconciliation.\n        \"\"\"\n        await self.publisher.publish(\n            event_name=\"instant.settlement.reconciliation.required\",\n            payload={\n                \"transaction_id\": command.transaction_id,\n                \"reservation_id\": reservation_id,\n                \"status\": \"TIMEOUT\",\n                \"requires_manual_check\": True,\n            },\n        )\n\n        # Mark transaction as PENDING_RECONCILIATION\n        raise InstantSettlementTimeout(\n            \"InstaPay timeout - queued for reconciliation\"\n        )\n```\n\n### 3.3 Wallet Service Compensation Handler\n\n```python\n# services/wallet_service/app/events/instant_settlement_consumer.py\n\nasync def handle_instant_settlement_compensation(\n    self,\n    event: InstantSettlementCompensation,\n) -> None:\n    \"\"\"\n    Handle compensation event - release reserved funds.\n    \"\"\"\n    reservation_id = event.data[\"reservation_id\"]\n\n    # Mark the PENDING ledger entry as REVERSED\n    await self.ledger_repo.mark_reversed(\n        entry_ids=[reservation_id],\n        reason=event.data[\"reason\"],\n        failed=True,\n    )\n\n    # Publish compensation complete event\n    await self.publisher.publish(\n        \"ledger.reservation.released\",\n        {\n            \"reservation_id\": reservation_id,\n            \"transaction_id\": event.data[\"transaction_id\"],\n            \"released_at\": datetime.utcnow().isoformat(),\n        },\n    )\n```\n\n---\n\n## 4. Sequence Diagram: Successful Instant Settlement\n\n```mermaid\nsequenceDiagram\n    autonumber\n    actor User\n    participant API as API Gateway\n    participant TXN as Transaction Service<br/>(SettlementRouter)\n    participant INST as InstantSettlement<br/>Strategy\n    participant RISK as Risk & Compliance<br/>Service\n    participant WALLET as Wallet Service\n    participant INSTAPAY as InstaPay<br/>(External)\n    participant KAFKA as Event Bus<br/>(Kafka)\n\n    User->>API: POST /transactions<br/>{settlement_type: \"INSTANT\"}\n    API->>TXN: route(PaymentCommand)\n\n    Note over TXN: Determine strategy based on<br/>user tier & eligibility\n\n    TXN->>INST: execute(InstantPaymentCommand)\n\n    rect rgb(240, 248, 255)\n        Note over INST,RISK: Step 1: Synchronous Risk Check\n        INST->>RISK: POST /api/v1/assessment/instant\n        RISK-->>INST: {decision: APPROVED, score: 0.2}\n    end\n\n    rect rgb(240, 255, 240)\n        Note over INST,WALLET: Step 2: Reserve Funds\n        INST->>WALLET: POST /wallet/v1/reserve\n        WALLET-->>INST: {reservation_id: \"res_123\",<br/>status: PENDING}\n    end\n\n    rect rgb(255, 248, 240)\n        Note over INST,INSTAPAY: Step 3: Synchronous External Settlement\n        INST->>INSTAPAY: POST /v1/instant-settle\n        Note over INSTAPAY: Process settlement<br/>in real-time\n        INSTAPAY-->>INST: {reference: \"IP_456\",<br/>status: SETTLED}\n    end\n\n    rect rgb(248, 240, 255)\n        Note over INST,KAFKA: Step 4: Publish Success Event\n        INST->>KAFKA: publish(InstantSettlementSucceeded)\n        KAFKA-->>INST: ack\n    end\n\n    INST-->>TXN: SettlementResult{COMPLETED}\n    TXN-->>API: 200 OK\n    API-->>User: Transaction Settled<br/>{instapay_ref: \"IP_456\"}\n\n    Note over KAFKA,WALLET: Async: Event-Driven Ledger Update\n\n    KAFKA->>WALLET: consume(InstantSettlementSucceeded)\n\n    rect rgb(240, 255, 240)\n        Note over WALLET: Finalize Ledger Entries\n        WALLET->>WALLET: mark_committed(debit_entry)\n        WALLET->>WALLET: create_credit_entry(COMMITTED)\n    end\n\n    WALLET->>KAFKA: publish(ledger.instant.settled)\n```\n\n---\n\n## 5. Implementation Roadmap\n\n### Phase 1: Foundation (Sprint 1-2)\n- [ ] Add `settlement_type` field to `PaymentCommand` model\n- [ ] Implement `SettlementRouter` with strategy factory\n- [ ] Create `StandardSettlementStrategy` wrapper around existing `PaymentSaga`\n- [ ] Add database migrations for instant settlement metadata\n\n### Phase 2: InstaPay Integration (Sprint 3-4)\n- [ ] Implement `InstaPayClient` with circuit breaker\n- [ ] Create `InstantSettlementStrategy` class\n- [ ] Add InstaPay configuration and credentials management\n- [ ] Implement synchronous risk assessment endpoint\n\n### Phase 3: Wallet Service Updates (Sprint 4-5)\n- [ ] Add `InstantSettlementSucceeded` event handler\n- [ ] Implement compensation handlers for fund release\n- [ ] Add new event types to consumer registry\n- [ ] Update ledger service for instant settlement entries\n\n### Phase 4: Risk & Compliance (Sprint 5-6)\n- [ ] Create instant settlement rule set\n- [ ] Implement velocity checking for instant settlements\n- [ ] Add synchronous assessment endpoint\n- [ ] Configure stricter thresholds for instant payments\n\n### Phase 5: Testing & Rollout (Sprint 6-7)\n- [ ] Integration tests with InstaPay sandbox\n- [ ] Chaos testing for failure scenarios\n- [ ] Performance testing for latency SLAs\n- [ ] Gradual rollout with feature flags\n\n---\n\n## 6. Summary of Key Architectural Decisions\n\n| Decision | Rationale |\n|----------|-----------|\n| **Separate Strategy** instead of modifying Saga | Preserves async resilience of existing flow; avoids anti-pattern of blocking calls in Saga |\n| **Synchronous Risk Check** for instant path | Instant settlements require immediate decision; cannot wait for async background processing |\n| **Event-driven Ledger Update** | Preserves event-sourcing integrity; wallet service remains single source of truth for ledger |\n| **Compensation via Events** | Ensures auditability; enables retry and idempotency; aligns with existing patterns |\n| **Circuit Breaker** for InstaPay | Protects system from external service degradation; enables graceful fallback |\n| **Stricter Risk Rules** for instant | Irrevocable nature of instant settlements requires higher scrutiny |\n\n---\n\n## 7.
